Growth in other advanced economies has also been
more resilient than expected, prompting upward

revisions to near-term forecasts. AI-related investment
and stronger underlying momentum have supported
activity in the United States, while defence spending and
broader resilience across services and investment have
contributed to growth in the euro area. Activity in the
United Kingdom has also held up better than expected,
supported by business investment. These tailwinds
across advanced economies have so far offset some of
the drag to activity from higher energy costs. However,

external forecasters and central banks expect
persistently elevated energy costs to reduce household
purchasing power, raise firms’ costs and increasingly
weigh on consumption and investment. In some
advanced economies, tighter financial conditions will
also weigh on growth.
3.2 The global outlook
Chapter 3  |  OutlookOutlookOutlookOutlookOutlookOutlookOutlook
Reserve Bank of Australia  |  Statement on Monetary Policy  |  November 2026Statement on Monetary Policy  |  November 2026Statement on Monetary Policy  |  November 2026Statement on Monetary Policy  |  November 2026Statement on Monetary Policy  |  November 2026Statement on Monetary Policy  |  November 2026Statement on Monetary Policy  |  November 2026 66

### Page 70

The outlook for GDP growth in China is broadly
unchanged from the August Statement, though
there have been some changes to the expected
timing and composition of growth. GDP growth is
forecast to be 4.6 per cent in 2026, before easing slightly
to 4.5 per cent in 2027 and 4.4 per cent in 2028. The
pick-up in growth in the September quarter is expected
to continue in the near term, given the announcement
of new policy support for infrastructure investment and

signals from authorities that supporting economic
activity will be elevated as a near-term policy priority.
Infrastructure investment is now expected to recover
from late 2026 as this support takes effect, while
structural headwinds are expected to continue weighing
on domestic demand more broadly. The timing and
effectiveness of the policy support remain uncertain,
particularly given high local government debt and a
range of barriers to some local governments

commencing infrastructure projects.
Longer-term government bond yields have risen
across advanced economies, alongside resilient
activity and persistent inflation. The increase appears
to reflect a combination of higher expected near-term
policy rates and higher longer-term real rates, reflecting
a mix of higher term premia and potentially higher
neutral rates. Market-based measures of long-term
inflation expectations remain consistent with central

bank targets. Strong investment and public demand
may be placing upward pressure on real rates, and
expectations of stronger future productivity growth –
including from the adoption of AI – may also be
contributing. Higher public and private debt issuance
and uncertainty about inflation and fiscal trajectories
may also be adding to the compensation investors
require to hold longer-duration bonds (see Box A: How
Does an Increase in Longer-term Real Yields Affect

Financial Conditions? and Box B: Developments in the
Neutral Rate of Interest).
The global inflation outlook has been revised higher,
reflecting an increase in energy prices and
continued resilience in global demand. Consensus
forecasters expect headline inflation to remain above
central bank targets into 2027 in many advanced
economies. Services disinflation has stalled, while core
goods inflation has picked up. The outlook for globally
traded goods inflation has been revised higher,

reflecting higher commodity prices and further strong
increases in export prices for semiconductors and other
AI-related goods across parts of Asia. AI-related price
pressures have not broadened materially beyond
electronics but could become more persistent or spread
to other parts of global supply chains if strong demand
continues to run ahead of the expansion in productive
capacity (see Key risk #2). The further increase in energy
prices since August could add to these upstream cost

pressures, although the extent and timing of
pass-through to consumer prices remains uncertain.
Overall, we continue to judge that the risks to global
inflation remain skewed to the upside.

Australian GDP growth is expected
to remain below our estimate of
potential growth throughout the
forecast period.
GDP growth has slowed since the end of 2025 and is
expected to remain subdued over the next couple of
years (Graph 3.3). The expected slowing in growth over
2026 reflects the effects of several headwinds weighing
on private activity, including the reduction in household
disposable incomes from higher inflation, weaker
conditions in the established housing market, and the

tightening in monetary policy, which more than offset
very strong data centre-related investment. Growth is
expected to remain below potential over the forecast
period, reflecting an assumption that financial
conditions remain restrictive (see Key judgement #2).
Consistent with the latest state and federal budgets,
public demand is expected to continue supporting
growth over the forecast period.
The risks to activity appear to be broadly balanced.

Stronger-than-expected global growth and further
strength in AI and data centre-related activity pose
upside risks to domestic growth (see Key risk #2).
However, historically low consumer and business
sentiment could be pointing to the risk that the
combined effect on domestic activity of the Middle East
conflict, the decline in housing prices and the tightening
in monetary policy could be more than assumed (see
Key risk #3).
The level of GDP at end-2028 is expected to be

broadly unchanged from the August forecasts.
Growth is projected to be slightly stronger than
expected in the near term, reflecting a much stronger
outlook for data centre-related investment compared
with the August Statement. Weighing against that is the
higher assumed market path for the cash rate, a weaker
outlook for the housing market and higher oil prices.
These pull growth in the latter part of the forecast period
a little below that expected in the August Statement.

Business investment is expected to increase strongly
and the outlook has been revised up significantly,

largely driven by data centre-related investment.
Business investment was stronger than expected in the
June quarter and firms’ surveyed expectations of future
investment were again revised higher. External
projections for the expected increase in Australia’s data
centre capacity through to 2030 have also continued to
be revised up as the size of the development pipeline
has increased. Our forecast for data centre investment is
in line with recent baseline external projections, but

below projections that assume Australia becomes a
significant regional hub for data centres. There is
considerable uncertainty around the extent of data
centre investment over coming years. This will depend
on a range of factors such as government policy,
community expectations, future demand for AI services
and grid capacity. We continue to expect that much of
this additional investment will be imported (around
70 per cent), limiting the boost to GDP growth. However,

data centre construction, as well as any potential
spillovers to higher investment in energy and utilities
infrastructure, will require domestically sourced labour
and materials. At this stage, most of this investment
pipeline is in New South Wales and Victoria where liaison
information suggests capacity constraints in the
construction sector are somewhat less binding.
3.3 Domestic outlook

### Page 72

At the same time, a higher assumed cash rate path, a
more significant easing in the established housing
market and higher global oil prices are expected to
weigh more on activity than in the August forecasts.
The forecasts are conditioned on a higher market path
for interest rates relative to August. In addition,
established housing prices have continued to fall more
quickly than expected, with external forecasters now
expecting a deeper downturn than assumed a few

months ago. The outlook for oil prices is also much
higher than assumed in August, which will weigh on real
household incomes. Reflecting these factors, the outlook
for household consumption and dwelling investment
has been revised lower, as has the outlook for business
investment unrelated to data centres.
The labour market is expected to
continue easing over the forecast
period, consistent with
below-potential GDP growth.
The unemployment rate is forecast to increase to

5.1 per cent by the end of 2028, which is around
1⁄4 percentage point higher than previously
expected (Graph 3.4). The unemployment rate has risen
by more than expected over recent months, and the
upward revision to forecast relative to the August
Statement largely reflects this higher starting point.
Elevated cost-of-living pressures appear to have
encouraged workers to enter or remain in the labour
force to a greater extent in recent months than was

expected at the time of the August Statement. These
pressures are expected to continue to support
participation for some time. The participation rate is
expected to decline very modestly by the end of 2028 as
easing cyclical pressures are expected to more than
offset the longer-run upward trend in female
participation. A slightly weaker outlook for demand later
in the forecast period is also contributing a little to the
upward revision to the unemployment rate profile by

late 2028.

As in previous forecasts, the
employment-to-population ratio is expected to
decline through to late 2028. Employment growth is

expected to remain broadly stable over the next year, in
line with the leading indicators of labour demand. From
mid-2027, employment growth is expected to be a little
weaker than in the August Statement, reflecting the
weaker outlook for GDP growth later in the forecast
period. The forecast employment-to-population ratio is
similar to the August Statement in the near term, before
declining more quickly to reach a lower level by the end
of the forecast period.

Reserve Bank of Australia  |  Statement on Monetary Policy  |  November 2026Statement on Monetary Policy  |  November 2026Statement on Monetary Policy  |  November 2026Statement on Monetary Policy  |  November 2026Statement on Monetary Policy  |  November 2026Statement on Monetary Policy  |  November 2026Statement on Monetary Policy  |  November 2026 69

### Page 73

The economy is expected to move
into balance in 2027, with spare
capacity then emerging in the
economy over the rest of the forecast
period.
Below-potential output growth and an easing in
labour market conditions are expected to see the
economy move from a position of excess demand to
around balance in 2027, before some spare capacity
emerges. The timing of the return to balance is slightly
later than in the August Statement, as a result of the
upgrade to near-term GDP growth.

However, there is considerable uncertainty around
the assessment of when the economy will return to
balance and the extent of spare capacity that
emerges. The impact of conflict-related costs on
inflation over the period ahead will also make it more
difficult to assess the degree of underlying capacity
pressures in the domestic economy. Our assumption for
potential output growth over the next couple of years
assumes productivity growth will pick up gradually (see

Key risk #4). However, with the level of labour
productivity remaining around the same rate as prior to
the pandemic – implying near-zero productivity growth
on average over that period – continued weak
productivity outcomes may be constraining the ability of
firms to grow their supply capacity without generating
inflationary pressure.
Wages growth is expected to pick up
a little in the near term, then
moderate gradually from 2027 as
labour market conditions ease.

The forecast for year-ended wages growth has been
revised a little higher across the forecast period. In
the near term, nominal wages growth is expected to be
supported by the effect of higher-than-expected
capacity pressures in the first half of the year as well as
the Fair Work Commission’s award wage decision (which
had already been incorporated in the August forecasts).
The influence of both higher-than-expected inflation
outcomes and inflation expectations on wage

negotiations is expected to add further upward pressure
on wages growth relative to the August forecast. Some
large wage increases in enterprise bargaining
agreements are expected to support wages growth in
the near term, although the timing of pay increases for
these agreements may contribute to volatility in
quarterly outcomes, particularly for the public sector.
Wages growth expectations from firms in the RBA’s
liaison program are elevated and suggest little

easing over the year ahead (see Box C: Insights from
Liaison). This is consistent with the central forecast for
wages growth. Growth is expected to moderate from
mid-2027 onwards, reflecting easing labour market
conditions and lower inflation outcomes. Wages growth
is expected to have eased to 2.9 per cent in year-ended
terms by end-2028 (Graph 3.5).

Unit labour cost growth is forecast to
be elevated in the near term, before
easing over the second half of the
forecast period.
Year-ended growth in unit labour costs (ULCs) is
expected to increase in coming quarters, reflecting
weak productivity growth and higher wages growth.
Year-ended growth in ULCs is then expected to
moderate from late 2027 onwards, alongside slowing
nominal wages growth and the pick-up in productivity
growth to its assumed medium-term trend growth rate

of 0.7 per cent per year (Graph 3.6). Nominal ULCs are
the measure of labour costs most relevant for firms’ cost
of production and so for inflation outcomes. Year-ended
ULCs growth is expected to remain above 2.5 per cent –
the rate that is consistent with inflation being
sustainably at target – until late in the forecast period.

The forecast for underlying inflation
has been revised higher over the
next year, mainly owing to higher
conflict-related cost pressures and
higher-than-expected domestic
capacity pressures.
Trimmed mean inflation is expected to remain
above 3 per cent until the end of 2027, easing
gradually to reach 21⁄2 per cent around mid-2028
(Graph 3.7). Year-ended underlying inflation is expected

to remain elevated at a little above 31⁄2 per cent until
mid-2027, reflecting existing capacity pressures in the
economy and the pass-through of higher
conflict-related costs to consumer prices. Elevated
inflation expectations are also contributing to
underlying inflation over the coming year or so. From
mid-2027 onwards, inflation is expected to ease due to
the emergence of spare capacity in the economy and
labour market and as conflict-related cost effects begin

to unwind.

### Page 75

Cost pressures related to the Middle East conflict are
expected to place more upward pressure on
underlying inflation over the year ahead than
previously assumed. A possible re-escalation in the
conflict had been flagged as an upside risk to inflation in
the August Statement. Since then, oil and fuel prices
have increased by much more than had been assumed
and are now assumed to remain at a higher level over
the forecast period. We expect higher global energy

prices will contribute to higher domestic inflationary
pressures in late 2026 and early 2027, even as domestic
activity slows and the labour market eases (Graph 3.8).
The judgement around the degree of pass-through from
global energy prices to domestic prices is broadly similar
to what had been assumed in August. It is consistent
with information from liaison and the Business Outlook
Scenario Survey (BOSS) in September, where firms
reported plans to continue raising prices in response to

past cost increases arising partially from the conflict.
From mid-2027 onwards, we expect cost effects to
unwind in net terms, albeit to a lesser extent than
assumed in the August Statement as oil prices are now
assumed to remain at a higher level than previously
expected and refined margins are assumed to remain
elevated for longer (see Key judgement #1). The balance
of risks to conflict-related inflationary pressures remains
skewed to the upside, particularly as crude oil and

refined fuel prices could remain elevated or rise further
(see Key risk #1).
Relative to the August forecasts, domestic capacity
pressures are also expected to exert a bit more
upward pressure on inflation over the year ahead.
We now judge that stronger-than-expected domestic
capacity pressures explain more of the recent
unexpected strength in underlying inflation (see
Chapter 2: Economic Conditions). Those higher capacity
pressures are also expected to contribute a little more to

inflation over the year ahead. This upward pressure is
expected to ease gradually over the forecast period, as
spare capacity emerges in the economy and the
labour market.

Elevated inflation expectations are expected to

contribute to inflationary pressure over the forecast
period, supporting the pass-through of firms’ costs
to consumer prices. Several measures of short- and
medium-term inflation expectations have increased
alongside the recent increases in fuel prices, and some
measures of longer-term expectations have also drifted
higher (see Chapter 2: Economic Conditions). We judge
that elevated expectations will contribute to higher
realised inflation by supporting expected cost growth,

making firms more willing to pass these higher costs
through to prices, and placing upward pressure on
wages growth. If the conflict in the Middle East is more
protracted than assumed in our baseline forecasts, there
is a risk that inflation expectations may increase further
(see Key risk #1).

Headline inflation has been revised
higher in the near term due to
stronger growth in fuel prices and
higher underlying inflation.
Headline inflation is expected to remain above 3 per
cent until late 2027, which is around three quarters
longer than in the August forecast, and reach the
midpoint of the 2–3 per cent inflation target range a
bit later than expected in August (Graph 3.9). Headline
inflation is expected to peak at 4.5 per cent in the
December quarter this year due to

higher-than-expected fuel prices. The assumed decline
in global oil prices is expected to put downward
pressure on headline inflation from early 2027. Over the
remainder of the forecast period, headline inflation has
been revised higher relative to our August forecasts,
reflecting higher underlying inflation (Graph 3.10).
Headline inflation is expected to reach the midpoint of
the target in mid-2028 as capacity pressures and
conflict-related costs ease.

Reserve Bank of Australia  |  Statement on Monetary Policy  |  November 2026Statement on Monetary Policy  |  November 2026Statement on Monetary Policy  |  November 2026Statement on Monetary Policy  |  November 2026Statement on Monetary Policy  |  November 2026Statement on Monetary Policy  |  November 2026Statement on Monetary Policy  |  November 2026 73

### Page 77

The staff assess that the risks to the domestic inflation
outlook are skewed to the upside, despite becoming
more balanced since the August Statement as some of
the risks identified then have been incorporated into the
central forecast. In August, we identified three main
sources of upside risk to the global and domestic
inflation outlook: a re-escalation of the Middle East
conflict; domestic capacity pressures being greater than
assumed; and the global AI boom generating more of an

inflationary impetus than assumed. Since then, the risk
of a re-escalation of the Middle East conflict has
crystallised and staff have re-evaluated the extent of
domestic capacity constraints; both of which have led to
upward revisions to the central inflation forecast over the
next year. However, there is still uncertainty around the
outlook for global energy prices, the role of inflation
expectations in price and wage dynamics as well as the
impact on inflation from AI activity.

The staff also identified in August that there were upside
risks to global and domestic economic activity from
AI-related demand, but that domestic economic activity
could be weaker if the Middle East conflict and the
decline in housing prices affected the economy by more
than assumed in the central forecast. These remain key
risks to the outlook.
Key risk #1 – High inflation could be
more persistent if energy prices
remain elevated, or if inflation

expectations increase or start to
have a greater bearing on wage- and
price-setting.
We have modelled a scenario where crude oil
remains around US$100 per barrel and refined
product prices remain at elevated levels over the
forecast period. Many external forecasters are revising
higher their outlook for crude oil and refined product
prices, on concerns that some of the key margins of
adjustment in global energy markets that have helped
to cushion the impact of the disruption to oil supply and

global activity are reaching their limits (see Chapter 2:
Economic Conditions and Key judgement #1).
Persistently higher global oil prices would leave
domestic inflation higher for a longer period than in
the central forecast. Higher oil prices would directly
increase fuel prices and firms’ input costs, with the latter
gradually passed through to consumer prices as firms
adjust to persistently higher costs. By contrast, the
central forecast assumes a decline in oil prices, which

starts to ease cost pressures related to the Middle East
conflict from mid-2027, contributing to lower inflation
over time (Graph 3.11). Higher inflation also raises
inflation expectations in the scenario, reinforcing
inflationary pressures over the forecast period. Assuming
that the cash rate and exchange rate follow the same
path that underpins the central forecasts, underlying
inflation would be around 23⁄4 per cent by
end-2028 rather than being around the 21⁄2 per cent.

3.4 Key risks to the outlook
Chapter 3  |  OutlookOutlookOutlookOutlookOutlookOutlookOutlook
Reserve Bank of Australia  |  Statement on Monetary Policy  |  November 2026Statement on Monetary Policy  |  November 2026Statement on Monetary Policy  |  November 2026Statement on Monetary Policy  |  November 2026Statement on Monetary Policy  |  November 2026Statement on Monetary Policy  |  November 2026Statement on Monetary Policy  |  November 2026 74

### Page 78

It is possible that inflation expectations could rise
even further or that inflation expectations provide
greater impetus to wages growth and inflation
through second-round effects. The central forecasts
and scenario assume that the increase in households’
and firms’ inflation expectations do exert some upward
pressure on inflation and wages growth. However, given
households and firms are paying particularly close
attention to inflation, it is possible that inflation

expectations will influence price- and wage-setting
decisions by more than assumed. This heightened
attentiveness to inflation could also make expectations
more persistent and more responsive to realised
inflation. With headline inflation forecast to rise further in
the near term, this increases the risk that inflation
expectations remain higher for longer than assumed in
our central forecast.
Key risk #2 – The global AI demand
boom may continue to drive up

global growth and inflation.
MTP growth may not moderate as expected over the
next few years if AI-demand growth continues at the
same pace. It is possible that growth forecasts could
continue to be upwardly revised by Consensus
forecasters if the current upswing in AI-related
investment (and spillovers associated with the boom) is
more persistent than currently assumed. The rise in
interest rates across the yield curve in many advanced
economies may reflect higher neutral rates – that is,

expectations that demand will prove more resilient and
inflation more persistent than currently assumed. Higher
longer-term real rates may partly reflect expectations of
stronger future productivity growth, including from the
adoption of AI. Further investment in anticipation of
higher productivity could also support demand and add
to capacity pressures over the next few years, while most
of the productivity gains are likely to manifest beyond
the next two years (at least in Australia).

The ramp-up of investment in data centres may also
contribute to capacity and pricing pressures
domestically, particularly by drawing on resources in
the construction industry. These price pressures could
be stronger than currently expected as the forecast
pipeline of investment is worked through, or if
investment proves stronger than expected.
Key risk #3 – Domestic economic
activity could be weaker than
expected.
It is possible that the headwinds affecting

households and businesses over the next year could
have a larger adverse effect than assumed. The
central forecasts assume that higher oil prices, higher
interest rates and lower housing prices all weigh on
domestic economic activity broadly in line with average
historical relationships. Timely indicators of spending
remain consistent with this assumption, with no
evidence of a material slowing in consumption growth
in recent months. However, the very weak levels of

consumer and business sentiment could be pointing to
a more pronounced slowing in household and business
spending growth than assumed.
We have calibrated a downside domestic activity
scenario where there is a larger response in
household and business spending to these
headwinds. There is a range of estimates for the effects
of these headwinds in the literature, highlighting the
uncertainty around their impact on economic activity.
Drawing on this evidence, the scenario uses larger

responses than assumed in the central forecast, thereby
identifying a plausible downside outlook for domestic
activity. In the scenario, firms feel forced to pass on the
conflict-related costs to prices as in the central forecast;
but households respond more cautiously to the
resultant pressures on real income and wealth, reducing
discretionary consumption by more than assumed in the
central forecast. Such an effect on spending might be
compounded by a tightening in borrowing constraints

and incentives for housing investors. The scenario
assumes firms respond to weaker demand and margin
pressure with a sharper pullback on (non-data centre)
investment and hiring. Overall, the level of GDP is around
1.0 per cent lower than the central forecasts
by end-2028.
The larger pullback in household and business
spending results in weaker activity and employment
growth, but only modestly lower inflation than in
the central forecast, assuming interest rates and the

exchange rate follow the same path as the central
forecast. The greater caution exhibited by households
pushes the gross household saving ratio up gradually to
a peak of around 17 per cent, where it remains over the
remainder of the forecast period. The greater reduction

in hiring pushes the unemployment rate 0.3 percentage
points higher by end-2028, creating more spare capacity
in the domestic economy than in the central forecast
(Graph 3.12). The additional spare capacity in the
economy and the labour market reduces inflationary
pressures somewhat, but the assumption of cost
pass-through by firms limits this effect. Overall, trimmed
mean inflation reaches 21⁄2 per cent only one quarter
earlier than in the central forecast.

Key risk #4 – There remains
considerable uncertainty about the
degree of capacity pressures in the
domestic economy and labour
market.
Despite the recent reassessment, it is possible that

we are still understating the extent of capacity
pressures. While we assess that capacity constraints in
the economy and labour market have been tighter than
previously judged, model-based estimates of the
unemployment rate that is consistent with low and
stable inflation continue to point to greater tightness in
labour market conditions than our central assessment. In
addition, if productivity growth were to remain subdued
over the forecast period, rather than pick up modestly as

assumed, it may suggest that the economy’s supply
capacity would be lower than currently expected. The
implications for inflation would depend on how
households and businesses adjust their spending
and expectations.
Conversely, capacity pressures may be weaker than
expected. The broader suite of full employment
indicators points to somewhat less labour market
tightness than our central assessment. It is also possible
that more of the recent strength in wages growth and

inflation reflects factors other than excess demand, such
as conflict-related cost effects or higher inflation
expectations. In these cases, wages growth and inflation
may moderate more quickly than in the central forecast.
Productivity growth could also recover more quickly
than assumed, including if the anticipated benefits of AI
adoption and other technological developments are
realised sooner.
There is also a risk that the unemployment rate
increases by more than expected if labour supply

remains stronger than assumed. There is a risk that
elevated cost-of-living pressures could support labour
force participation for longer, or to a greater extent, than
assumed. If so, stronger labour supply could result in a
higher unemployment rate than forecast, even if labour
demand evolves broadly as expected.

Document 335: How the blue wave could reshape US energy and data centres

Source PDF: all report pdf/How the blue wave could reshape US energy and data centres.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-11-05
Pages: 9
Status: ok

### Page 1

Energy aﬀordability shaped the midterms. A divided
government will now face pressure to shield consumers from
rising data centre power costs while speeding up new energy
supply. Onshoring and trade protections will continue, despite
higher near-term costs, as energy security remains a priority
Coco Zhang
After the midterm elections, the government will need to minimise data
centre-related costs for consumers, bring new power supply online faster and

protect supply chains. Pictured: Data centre in Ohio
Exit polls from the US midterm elections highlighted a key concern
shaping voters’ decisions: energy aﬀordability. Nearly 90% of
voters said the cost of living was a crucial or major factor in their
vote for Congress, with many pointing to gasoline and utility costs.
Meanwhile, as the AI data centre boom strains power supply and
infrastructure, 56% said the issue was a crucial or major factor in

their vote.
With Democrats now set to control both the House and Senate,
what does this mean for US energy and data centre policy?

### Page 2

Newly elected lawmakers will face pressure to address these
concerns, requiring the Democratic Congress and Republican White
House to ﬁnd common ground on shielding consumers from data
centre-related costs and accelerating new power supply, including
through permitting reform. Onshoring and supply chain protections
are also likely to continue. While they can raise near-term costs,
they remain important to US energy security. Developments in all
three areas could have signiﬁcant implications for businesses.

That said, Congress needs to strike a balance when drafting
legislation aimed at achieving these goals. More aggressive
measures could face a veto from President Trump.
Strengthened data centre regulation ahead,
but the devil is in the detail
The data centre industry is facing heightened local opposition.
Recent polls suggest that around one in seven Americans is
concerned about or opposes data centre development in their
neighbourhoods.
In response to this issue, there is likely to be tighter data centre

regulation. State-level policy will be more important to watch, as
action is politically and practically easier at this level. The most
likely approach is targeted regulation, much of which is already
emerging. This includes data centre-related taxes, reduced tax
incentives, environmental standards, and requirements for
developers to bear relevant cost increases. Outright moratoriums
remain unlikely to become mainstream. Where they are introduced,

they are more likely to be temporary, giving states time to develop
a data centre policy framework.
At the federal level, the key unanswered question is how much
authority should remain with the states and whether federal
legislation should mandate state regulations or simply provide
guidance. Republicans generally favour letting states lead, while
Democrats tend to support a stronger federal role.
We think sweeping data centre legislation is unlikely because of

President Trump’s veto power. Federal action is more likely to focus
Your privacy is important

our Cookie policy.

### Page 3

on aﬀordability by protecting electricity customers from higher
costs and formalising data centres' responsibility to help fund grid
and energy infrastructure upgrades. Such measures could be added
to broader legislation, such as must-pass spending bills needed to
avoid a government shutdown, or a bipartisan package like
permitting reform.
More broadly on AI, we expect the Democratic Party to increase
scrutiny of technology companies, as discussed here, while utilising

the next two years to prepare an AI governance framework for 2028
and beyond.
However, tighter AI and data centre regulation is unlikely to
fundamentally alter the industry’s growth trajectory. AI’s
importance to US economic growth and competition with China will
remain powerful drivers of expansion.
Business implications
State-level policy would more directly aﬀect project siting,
which is important for data centre companies to watch.
Where state rules are tighter, project development could slow.

However, a more restrictive regulatory environment could
also increase the value of data centres already approved or
under construction, easing some of the industry’s capital
constraints. Higher valuations could also encourage
developers to be more selective and expand at a more
measured pace, ultimately improving the quality of the
project pipeline.
Developers should also treat community engagement as a
strategic investment. Recent surveys show that while only

20-30% of Americans support local data centre construction,
support can rise to 50-70% when there are accompanying
community investments, such as in public infrastructure
improvements or reductions in residents' costs. More
developers are now signing binding community beneﬁt
agreements as part of project development.

Enabling faster power generation through
permitting reform
Both parties support timely expansion of electricity supply to meet

rising demand from AI and manufacturing reshoring. They also
generally agree that energy abundance can help reduce cost
pressures. While Democrats and Republicans diﬀer on the preferred
energy mix, there is still common ground. Technologies likely to
retain bipartisan support include nuclear, geothermal, carbon
capture, and biofuels.
However, the greatest potential for policy action may lie not in
speciﬁc technologies, but in permitting reform.
Today, many energy projects can spend over ﬁve years navigating

the federal permitting process. Around $240bn to $280bn of
infrastructure investment across the eight sectors below enters the

permitting pipeline each year. Lengthy delays can therefore leave

With both parties recognising the need for reform, the Senate
introduced the Bipartisan American Aﬀordability and Jobs Act
(BAAJA) in September 2026. Key permitting proposals include:

Speeding up approvals for energy projects
Providing greater certainty and consistency across
administrations
Making it easier to permit power transmission lines
Despite debate over its environmental impact, there is broad
agreement that the BAAJA would meaningfully improve the
permitting process. The key question is whether it can become law
under the current Congress. The Senate is expected to vote on 9
November, after which the bill would still need approval from the

House and President Trump. Democrats may want to delay the bill
and seek changes once they regain control of Congress next year,
but doing so could increase the risk of a presidential veto. This
creates an incentive to reach a deal sooner rather than later.

### Page 6

Even if the BAAJA does not pass under the current Congress,
permitting reform is likely to remain a bipartisan theme. The
growing need to expand power supply and infrastructure more
quickly will keep pressure on future Congresses to streamline
permitting.
Business implications
For energy companies – both renewables and fossil fuels –
the BAAJA could signiﬁcantly shorten permitting timelines. If
enacted, it would give project developers greater certainty,

reducing the risk of litigation and permit cancellations. This
could further expand project siting options for developers.
Nevertheless, in the very near term, projects may need to
retain ﬂexibility around their permitting strategy while the
new policy framework is developed and implemented.
Expect more protectionism and reshoring
efforts against China dominance
From clean energy technologies to the critical minerals that
underpin much of the US economy, China controls a large share of

global production capacity. Consequently, reducing reliance on
China will remain a US policy priority. Such eﬀorts would increase
rather than reduce project costs in the near term. But the long-term
beneﬁts of stronger domestic manufacturing and more secure
supply chains could outweigh those costs.
Global supply chain capacity distribution
across various sectors

### Page 7

Source: ING Research, BloombergNEF, International Energy Agency
This creates two key risks. First, heavy reliance on China leaves US
industries vulnerable to geopolitical tensions. We have already seen
this with rare earths, where Chinese export restrictions contributed
to disruptions for US auto manufacturers. Second, China’s supply
chain dominance can put US manufacturers at a competitive
disadvantage.
Building more secure energy and natural resource supply chains

has long attracted bipartisan support in the US. The Trump
administration has supported domestic rare earth production
through funding, purchase agreements, and equity investments. In
solar, China-focused trade and tax credit restrictions are also
supporting the expansion of domestic manufacturing.
We expect this focus to continue. The executive branch could
pursue further protective measures and is unlikely to face major
opposition from Congress. Congress could also authorise additional

funding to strengthen rare earth supply chains.
Furthermore, a Democratic-controlled Congress could push to
extend clean manufacturing tax credits as part of broader
negotiations with President Trump. Given the administration’s focus
on countering China and expanding US manufacturing, an
extension could emerge as a compromise in a broader legislative
package under an optimistic scenario. All these eﬀorts would also
extend into future administrations.

This publication has been prepared by ING solely for information purposes

Business implications
For US companies:
Clean energy companies and other industries that rely on
rare earths, including semiconductors, AI, defence, and

electronics, should have enough policy certainty to build
long-term supplier relationships outside China. In the near
term, however, diversiﬁcation could raise supply chain costs
as alternative sources may be more expensive. Solar could
face particular cost pressures, as some tariﬀs on supply
chain equipment also apply to non-Chinese suppliers.
For overseas companies:
US eﬀorts to build supply chains outside China are creating
opportunities for manufacturers elsewhere. The US is

supporting rare earth production in Australia and Brazil, while
solar and battery suppliers in countries such as South Korea
and Japan could beneﬁt from stronger demand. This could
also encourage more manufacturing investment in the US.
Meanwhile, European clean energy developers could face
greater competition for non-Chinese supplies. While the EU
has not imposed US-style tariﬀs, it is tightening restrictions
on Chinese equipment through funding, procurement,

auction, and subsidy rules. This could intensify global
competition for non-Chinese suppliers and push costs higher.

黎光岱

M31 (6643 TT)

維持評等 
增加持股(Overweight) 
隨權利金營收比重提升，未來營運狀況更趨穩定 
⚫ 3Q26營收增幅低於預期 
⚫ 3Q26授權金與權利金季增福皆達雙位數 
⚫ 提升權利金比重，使整體營收分布更為平均 
3Q26營收增幅低於預期：M31 3Q26營收為 4.95億元(QoQ+17.4%，
YoY+39.6%)，以美金計價營收為 1,546 萬美元 (QoQ+16.2%，
YoY+31.0%)，營收成長受惠於客戶開案動能強勁、權利金穩健成長，
以及北美高價值專案營收認列。營業費用為 4.8 億元(QoQ+11.2%，
YoY+21.1%)，因調整部分獎金發放時點而使本季營業費用較高。營業
利益為 730萬元，相較 2Q26由虧轉盈。EPS為 0.35元。 
3Q26授權金與權利金季增福皆達雙位數：3Q26授權金收入為 4.06億
元(QoQ+17%，YoY+47%)，約佔營收 82%，創歷史單季第三高。Fabless
授權金收入以美元計算 QoQ+3%，YoY+127%，主要由北美客戶先進
製程專案及晶圓代工平台合作案帶動，其中包含公司首個 2奈米以下
的 GAA架構專案，Fabless客戶持續提升對 16奈米以下先進製程技術
的採用比例，先進製程專案已成為授權業務成長的主要驅動力。3Q26
權利金收入為 0.89億元(QoQ+19%，YoY+14%)，約佔營收 18%，創
1Q23以來新高，為近 15季最佳表現，Foundry權利金收入持續成長，
反映既有平台專案逐步放量並持續貢獻營收，未來 A14、2奈米及 CSP
等新案機會可望進一步延續公司於先進製程領域的成長動能。 
提升權利金比重，使整體營收分布更為平均：公司將於 4Q26持續深
化與北美 IDM客戶客製化記憶體平台、美系晶圓廠 12奈米平台的合
作，完成與大型晶圓廠 28奈米 Foundation IP合約簽署，2奈米以下
與多項先進製程專案持續開展，將帶動授權金維持高檔。公司目標
2026年 EPS轉正，且全年營收較 2025年成長。2027年授權金目標成
長約 10%，權利金挑戰 30%以上成長，隨著更多客戶專案逐步進入大
量生產階段，預期先進製程權利金收入將進入加速成長期，16奈米以
下先進製程權利金比重將提升至 50%。預期 2027年整體營收分布更
為平穩，目標上下半年分別為 45:55。 
我們基於 2027年預估 EPS 6.35元*60倍 PER，調整目標價至 381元，
維持增加持股評等。風險因子:專案進程與製程平台導入進度。

圖表三、營收地區比重

資料來源: M31，中信投顧整理 
3Q26美國市場營收較 2Q26成長超過一倍，營收占比從 27%提升至 41%，受惠於
數項高金額授權專案挹注帶動，包含全球網通供應商、行動晶片龍頭、IDM客戶
首案認列。中國市場從 40奈米升級至 16/12奈米甚至更先進節點，應用集中於 AI
邊緣裝置、車用晶片、AI與影像處理、機器人等，本季新增客戶涵蓋影像感測及
高速介面晶片設計業者。台灣市場維持穩健，主要需求集中於 MIPI 與 USB 等
Interface IP產品線，此外與台灣晶圓龍頭廠合作的 6奈米平台也依進度貢獻部分
授權金。整體營收來源維持高度多元化，中國、美國及台灣三大市場合計貢獻超
過 90%總營收。 
圖表四、客戶類型比重

資料來源: M31，中信投顧整理 
Fabless先進製程專案持續帶動授權動能，3Q26 16奈米以下製程節點貢獻 Fabless
客戶總授權金收入 75%，授權金收入主要集中於 8奈米以下先進製程節點，新開
案活動則以 12奈米及 22/28奈米平台最為活躍。Foundry 12~16奈米節點占 3Q26
授權金收入 83.1%，主要來自北美晶圓代工與 IDM客戶的新平台開發案件。先進
製程平台專案持續推展，受惠於領先晶圓代工客戶專案里程碑收入認列，帶動 8
奈米以下節點營收貢獻，與台灣龍頭合作的 6奈米製程平台有小量授權金認列。

投資評等說明  個股評等 Rating 定義 
買進 Buy (B) 相較於市場指數之回報潛力極具吸引力 
增加持股 Overweight (OW) 相較於市場指數之回報潛力略有吸引力 
中立 Neutral (N) 相較於市場指數之回報潛力相似 
降低持股 Underweight (UW) 相較於市場指數之回報潛力稍低 
賣出 Sell (S) 相較於市場指數之回報潛力較低 
未評等 Not Rated (NR)

Document 396: Token出海赚的钱，跟不上飙升的存储成本了

Source PDF: all report pdf/Token出海赚的钱，跟不上飙升的存储成本了.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-13
Pages: 6
Status: ok

### Page 1

⻔罗⻛投报告显⽰，美国企业开源模型⽀出中， 89% 流向中国模型，但开源模型仅占企业
AI 总⽀出的 4% 。随着 Agent 成为新增流量主⼒，⻓会话持续占⽤显存与存储， Token 收⼊
与硬件成本逐渐脱钩。存储涨价、⻓协采购与资产折旧进⼀步挤压利润，国产⼤模型出海
的竞争正从性能和价格转向存储效率与资产回报。
摘要：
硅⾕知名⻛险投资机构⻔罗⻛投（ Menlo Ventures ）发布的《 2026 年企业 AI 现状报告》显⽰，
美国企业⽤于开源模型的⽀出中， 89% 付给了中国模型。前三名中， Kimi 占 61% ， DeepSeek 占
15% ， GLM 占 11% 。
市场很愿意接受⼀套简洁叙事：依靠性能与定价，国产⼤模型抢⾛海外开发者预算。
但同⼀份报告⾥还有另⼀组数字：虽然开源模型在 Token 使⽤量上已经拿下了接近五分之⼀的份
额（ 19% ），但开源模型实际上仅占了企业模型总体⽀出的  4% ，绝⼤部分的企业 AI 预算依然流
向了闭源⼤模型。
换句话说， 89% 是⼀份浓度很⾼但体量有限的份额。

体量本⾝不是最⼤问题，真正的变化在于它的成本结构。要知道售卖词元表⾯是出售算⼒，本
质却是交付状态。⽽状态并不依附算⼒，它落脚在内存与存储，⽽这正是本轮涨价压⼒最集中
的元器件。
⽭盾由此显现：收⼊按词元核算，成本却跟着 GB ⾛。
流量在跑，资产在静置
当市场紧盯各⼤聚合平台持续更新的 Token 调⽤榜单，热议国产⼤模型在海外开源市场的份额之
争，多数分析仍将叙事锚在模型能⼒与报价优势上，默认这终究是⼀⻔ “ 算⼒换  Token” 的⽣意。
这套理解放在传统对话场景中尚可⾃洽，但在当下撑起海外新增流量的 Agent 任务⾥，负载的底
层逻辑正在出现分化。
传统对话推理对应的资源模型⾜够直观，也是⽀撑当下绝⼤多数 API 定价体系的基础。
在这套理想框架⾥：⽤⼾发起提问，推理集群完成计算并返回内容；⼀轮对话收尾、会话关
闭，本次请求占⽤的缓存资源随之释放，硬件回归空闲。
服务商可以依托 Token 总量估算成本，在集群利⽤率尚有空间时，吞吐越⾼越有机会摊薄单位开
销，低价跑量和以规模换利润的逻辑因⽽能够⾃洽， Token 数量与资源消耗也被默认处在相近的

增⻓节奏上。
⽽海外聚合渠道⾥拉动增量的流量主⼒，正在从零散的⼈机对话转向具备⾃动化链路的 Agent 任
务。
这类负载的资源节奏和普通问答存在明显分化：任务启动阶段，部分场景需要灌⼊⼏⼗万 Token
级别的前置⽂档与批量业务数据；之后进⼊数⼗轮链式⼯具调⽤，持续检索、读取外部信息；
整个会话可能挂起数⼩时乃⾄更久，同⼀份前置上下⽂会被反复复⽤。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

更关键的是在没有主动关闭会话的前提下，这部分上下⽂缓存会持续占⽤资源。模型持续⽣成
新 Token ，只是追加缓存并不会⾃动把这⼀块占⽤释放回资源池。
随着⻓会话 Agent 流量占⽐上升，约束点也在切换：⽐拼的不只是推理速度，系统可同时⽀撑的
常驻会话上限会成为新的紧约束。
⼚商对外宣传的百万级上下⽂窗⼝，更多是⾯向客⼾的产品卖点；落到机房的资源账⾯上，它
意味着⼀旦会话把窗⼝⽤起来，每⼀路活跃并发都可能需要预留数⼗ GB 的 HBM 空间⽤来承载持
续在线的会话状态。
市⾯上的 KV 量化、 MLA 以及各类缓存压缩技术，都在试图压低单会话的显存开销和抬升集群可
承载的并发规模。
但它们本质上只是优化单位成本，很难绕开那条底层约束：只要会话处于可随时响应的活跃状
态，它的核⼼状态数据就需要驻留在⾼速存储⾥。
本轮词元流量的扩张，核⼼增量不完全来⾃新⽣成的输出⽂本。
万亿级调⽤量很容易制造⼀种错觉：增⻓来⾃源源不断的新内容⽣成。实际上，有相当⼀部分
流量增⻓对应的是集群⾥持续堆叠、⻓期锁定且很难低成本跨任务调度回收的动态状态资产。

平台统计⾯板往往只记录进出的 Token 数量，但放到服务商的经营账本⾥，真正持续沉淀的成本
来⾃那些⻓期占⽤且很难低成本⼆次分配给其他业务的显存与⾼速内存。
⽭盾也由此显现：⾏业主流计价框架仍脱胎于瞬时算⼒时代的逻辑，收⼊按输⼊、输出词元计
量。但对应的资本开⽀和⾼速显存占⽤成本，却会随着并发会话的驻留时⻓持续累积。
有的 Agent 会⻓时间挂起运⾏⼀整天，单位时间产出词元很少且营收有限，显存却被持续占⽤。
它抬⾼了榜单上的调⽤规模，收⼊却跟不上资源的持续消耗，曾经趋于同步的两条增⻓曲线由
此拉开缺⼝。
这种成本与计费的错位，或许正在慢慢改写 Token 出海赛道的竞争底⾊。
此前赛道的竞争路径简单直接：压缩单 Token 算⼒开销，⽤低价拿量，借规模摊薄硬件投⼊。当
负载结构向 Agent 倾斜，固守吞吐和报价的单⼀竞争维度就可能撞上流量⾛⾼但利润收窄的陷
阱。
调⽤数字⾛⾼很容易带来业务向好的信号，但⼀部分服务商容易忽视不断累积的会话状态资产
正在构成对⽑利的持续侵蚀⻛险。
在热闹的份额榜单之外，状态资产的管理能⼒或许正在形成⼀条新的隐形分界线。

词元出海，看不⻅的显存负债
当存储成本抬升，市场很容易把它简化成存储芯⽚涨价的故事。  但放到推理集群运营与资本开
⽀的框架⾥看，元器件现货价格只是众多变量之⼀。
对利润构成持续性刚性约束的往往是⼀整套绑定供货、资产折旧、跨境流量与客⼾合规交织⽽
成的规则链条。

### Page 3

上游存储芯⽚正处在结构性紧平衡周期，服务器  DRAM 、⾯向 AI 集群的企业级 NAND 以及 HBM
的⻓期协议报价持续上⾏，原⼚整体库存维持在偏低区间。
如果想要拿到稳定供货，⾃建推理集群的服务商已经很难单纯依靠现货随买随⽤保障持续扩
容，往往需要提前签下季度乃⾄半年度量级的保底采购⻓单。
这也重塑了投⼊的性质：硬件采购不再是可以跟着业务节奏灵活调整的可变投⼊，转⽽变成带
有刚性履约约束的资本开⽀。
这类合约⼀旦敲定，即便后续海外流量⽆法充分填满集群容量，合约款项、资产折旧依旧要按
期兑现与计提，营收的波动很难对冲这部分刚性⽀出。
还有⼀块很容易被掩盖的成本，来⾃海外企业客⼾的合规留存约束。不少海外企业采购推理服
务时，会要求完整审计⽇志、调⽤链路乃⾄会话原始内容⻓期可追溯和可审计。
这部分持久化存储独⽴于推理时临时⽣成的 KV 缓存，会⻓期稳定占⽤存储容量。
⽽⼤部分标准化词元采购合同并不会单独列⽀这项存储服务费，对应的开销被摊⼊统⼀的 Token
报价，外部很难单独剥离识别。

即便依靠 KV 缓存压缩、量化⽅案以及迭代模型版本做优化，集群硬件的物理服役周期尚在，但
适配当前 Agent 负载的有效业务⽣命周期正在被持续压缩。
这迫使服务商下调资产预期回报周期，单位词元对应的折旧分摊随之⾛⾼。多重约束叠加，存
储早已不只是服务器的配套成本，在这类负载下，它正在逼近算⼒的权重，还经常成为集群扩
容的瓶颈。
存储对利润的侵蚀并⾮简单笼统的成本上涨，⽽是沿着多条独⽴路径持续压低集群的资产回
报。这类信号不会拆解展现在对外报价单中，⼤多只能在服务商后台的运维与经营报表⾥被观
测到。
⾸先是前缀缓存的命中率陷阱。前缀缓存的底层逻辑是以存储换取算⼒复⽤：缓存⾼频重复的
上下⽂前缀，⼀旦命中就可以跳过重复计算，压低单 Token 的算⼒开销。
但缓存本⾝附带写⼊开销、淘汰调度逻辑以及⽣命周期约束。
⼀旦流量⾛向⾼度碎⽚化、租⼾分散、请求随机性抬升，缓存命中率就可能快速跌破盈亏平衡
点。为缓存预留的⼤量显存持续占⽤资本却很难兑现预期的算⼒节约，进⽽拉低整套集群的资
产回报率。
其次是峰值保底带来的闲置折旧。为承接突发流量、规避会话被强制清理以及防⽌服务稳定性

下滑，运营侧往往需要预留⾜够的存储与显存冗余。
冗余资源⾯向尖峰⽽预留，常态⼤多闲置。冗余抬升带来更强的稳定性，代价是资产周转效率
下降。在跨境潮汐流量之下，这笔折旧成本持续刚性，难以跟随淡季词元营收同步回落。
最后是⻓会话带来的静默资源锁死。⼀部分 Agent ⾃动化任务有这样的特征：单会话词元产出速
率不⾼，但会话⽣命周期极⻓，持续占⽤⼀段显存空间。

### Page 4

这类负载不会迅速拉⾼平台吞吐榜单上的调⽤规模，却会逐步挤占显存资源，持续消耗集群的
扩容余量，有可能倒逼服务商提前启动新⼀轮硬件采购。
市场习惯于依靠词元单价和模型能⼒评判国产⼤模型出海的竞争格局，但在 Agent 主导的新流量
结构之下，竞争重⼼正在从单纯的算⼒⽐拼延伸向⼀套更精细的资产效率管理。
在⾏业⽬光仍停留在词元调⽤榜单时，存储资产周转、缓存盈亏线与折旧节奏正慢慢变成影响
出海业务盈亏的隐性标尺。
存储通胀重塑⼤模型出海⽣意？
存储成本上⾏的压⼒，在分销链路⾥划出⼀道明确分界：持有承载⻓驻会话硬件资产的玩家，
将承担最难以向外转嫁的刚性压⼒。
其中，部分服务商掌握调度策略和缓存规则，拥有⾯向下游报价的主动权，但对应的⻛险敞⼝
也最⾼。
不少⾃建集群的供给⽅需要依靠季度或半年度保底⻓协拿到稳定供货，⼤概率要独⾃承接芯⽚
报价上⾏、跨时区流量错配带来的资产闲置以及硬件快速迭代形成的减值压⼒。
在存储紧平衡和持续扩容的阶段，流量增⻓带来的新增营收很容易被持续追加的资本开⽀快速
吞噬。

另⼀种主流⽅式，是和海外云⼚商达成分成托管合作。从产业账本的视⻆，这常被理解为资产
负债的通俗出表：模型⽅输出模型授权，内存、持久化存储、跨时区运维以及合规⽇志留存对
应的硬件投⼊与资产⻛险，主要由云⼚商承接。
但这并不等同于会计准则意义上的资产剥离，也不是股权投资。如果合约设置了保底⽤量或成
本联动条款，部分成本压⼒依然有可能反向传导回来。
此外，还有路径是仅输出模型权重，完全依托聚合平台承接分发。这类供给⽅基本不持有推理
硬件，来⾃存储和算⼒资产的直接敞⼝最⼩，但议价能⼒通常也最弱。
平台掌握路由分配、流量限额、缓存策略的调整权限，供给⽅⼤多只能被动承接分配过来的流
量。即便可以通过版本和参数配置微调负载特征，也很难⾃主⼲预底层集群的资产效率。
没有绝对完美的路径，这些选择⼤多是在流量增⻓潜⼒与存储资产敞⼝之间做权衡，同时还要
兼顾合规、客⼾归属与定价主动权等其他约束。
在存储价格上⾏周期，资产出表对不少玩家⽽⾔会变成更有吸引⼒的选择；待到上游产能释放
和⻓协报价回落，⾃建集群的盈利窗⼝才会重新打开。

⾏业⾥层出不穷的 KV 量化、稀疏注意⼒、 CXL 内存池、分层缓存⽅案，很容易带来⼀种错觉：
依靠技术优化就能够抹平存储成本约束。
但落到运营账本上，各类⼯程⽅案都存在清晰的上限。它们可以拉⾼资产利⽤率，却⽆法根除
状态侧的存储刚需。
KV 压缩能够压低单会话显存占⽤，往往要以额外算⼒开销作为代价；稀疏注意⼒可以削减计算
量，却⽆法免除会话状态的驻留需求； CXL 内存池有助于分流显存压⼒，但服务器硬件改造本⾝

### Page 5

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
104 收藏 分享到 :
⼜会带来新⼀轮资本投⼊。
更关键的是优化收益⾼度绑定流量结构：上下⽂复⽤度⾼的 Agent 任务可以充分吃到缓存红利，
⽽碎⽚化和⼀次性的请求优化回报会快速衰减。
技术⼿段能够抬升资产效率的上限，却很难绕开会话状态必须依托硬件存储这⼀底层约束。
⻓期来看，存算分离架构有机会深度解耦计算与状态存储，实现会话状态跨节点灵活迁移，重
新塑造集群的资产配置逻辑。
但整套基础设施的重构链条漫⻓，很难在短期内扭转当下的成本格局。
相较于只体现规模的调⽤量榜单，另⼀组指标更能映照这笔⽣意的真实成⾊：⻓上下⽂请求占
⽐、缓存命中率、单会话平均存活时⻓、海外节点存储闲置率、存储⻓协季度报价。
⼀个朴素的检验标尺是：在调⽤量持续抬升的过程中，单位 Token ⽑利能否维持甚⾄改善。
如果营收持续上⾏，⽑利却⻓期横盘甚⾄⾛低，往往预⽰增量收益正在被存储折旧持续侵蚀。

词元出海正在发⽣⼀场安静的业态切换：早期它更容易被归⼊算⼒租赁⽣意，边际成本有望随
规模持续摊薄。⽽随着⻓会话 Agent 逐步成为流量主⼒，这项业务的属性正在向在线状态托管靠
拢。
显存与存储成为核⼼沉淀资产，边际成本很难持续下⾏，资产周转率直接决定利润底盘。
流量能够顺着聚合平台的路由随时迁移，但硬件资本开⽀⼀旦落地，折旧节奏不会跟随客流同
步调整。以流动性极强的短期订单，匹配⻓期计提折旧的重资产，⼆者天然存在收益与成本的
周期错配。
市场总在⽐拼词元调⽤总量与份额排名，但站在供给侧的账本上更值得追问的是：每新增⼀条
常驻会话需要锁定多少 GB 的存储，这份资产能不能在折旧周期内回笼⾜够的现⾦流。
份额是需求端投出的选票，存储资产账本才是决定⼤模型出海⽣意能否⻓期跑通的核⼼约束。
当词元逐步⾛向标准化⼤宗商品，最终拉开盈利差距的或许不再是⽤于⽣成⽂本的算⼒，⽽是
承载上下⽂记忆的存储资产。

### Page 1

Global trade will remain resilient in 2027, but the focus will
shift from tariﬀs to ownership, technology and investment.
While not our base case, an escalation in an EU-China trade
war would rank among the most signiﬁcant risks to global
trade

Inga Fechner and Carsten Brzeski
Europe is undoubtedly facing a second 'China shock' – only this time around,
it's largely negative
How to read our three calls for 2027
Source: ING
ING's base call: Trade survives politics

### Page 2

Global goods trade continues to weather geopolitical shocks.
Over the past few years, trade has proved far more resilient
than global headlines suggest. We expect that resilience to
continue into 2027. Disruptions in global shipping, shifting
tariﬀ regimes and recurring geopolitical tensions have
repeatedly altered trade routes without derailing trade itself.
Strong demand for AI-related investment and infrastructure
should continue to support trade ﬂows, particularly across

Asia, while selective US-China decoupling is likely to beneﬁt
third countries that attract production and investment. For
2027, we expect global goods trade to remain positive,
expanding about 3% year-on-year. In short, trade survives
politics in 2027 because companies can still adapt faster
than governments can restrict them.
Our risky call: Ownership matters more than
trade
Tariﬀs were only the ﬁrst step. By 2027, the real battleground shifts
to capital, technology and ownership. As companies invest behind

tariﬀ walls, governments stop merely screening new investment
and begin requiring companies to restructure, separate sensitive
operations or divest existing strategic assets.
A foreign-owned battery plant, semiconductor facility, or data
centre once supported for bringing local investment and jobs is
now treated as a national-security risk. Companies that once
navigated geopolitical tensions through diversiﬁcation ﬁnd
themselves forced to choose sides. The question is no longer who

exports the most, but who controls the productive capacity on
which economies depend. Trade would continue, but the corporate
structures that have kept it moving would start to fracture as
governments alter trade ﬂows. This could potentially cause global
goods trade growth to stall or even contract in 2027.
Our bold call: EU-China trade war

Included in the following bundle
The EU and China enter an outright trade conﬂict. Europe abandons

its largely open-market approach and increasingly applies
reciprocity principles to market access and investment. Chinese
ﬁrms investing in strategic sectors face mandatory 50:50 joint
ventures, local ownership requirements and growing restrictions on
acquisitions.
At the same time, the EU imposes broad tariﬀs on Chinese imports,
with rates reaching 50% in politically sensitive sectors such as
vehicles, chemicals, machinery and electronics. China retaliates

with measures of similar scale.
The consequences are signiﬁcant. China accounts for roughly 15%
of global goods trade, while the EU accounts for around 13.5%.
Together they represent almost 30% of world goods trade. With the
EU being China's largest trading partner in goods and China being
the EU's second-largest trading partner in goods, a full-scale trade
conﬂict would rank among the most consequential economic
conﬂicts of the modern era.

Document 480: スペインは解散総選挙へ

Source PDF: all report pdf/スペインは解散総選挙へ.pdf
Report source: MURC
Date: 2026-10-07
Pages: 4
Status: ok

### Page 1

2026 年 10 月 7 日

MURC Focus

スペインは解散総選挙へ

～右派連立政権が成立する見込み～

調査部 主任研究員 土田 陽介

1. 解散総選挙に臨んだサンチェス首相 
スペインのペドロ・サンチェス首相が 10 月 5 日、議会を解散し、11 月 29 日に総選挙（定数 350、比例代表制）
を行うと発表した。住宅価格や家賃の高騰に対する政府の緊急政令案が、10 月 2 日に下院で否決されたことを
受けて、少数与党による政権運営の行き詰まりを意識した首相が、国民に信を問う決断をした形である。ただし、
サンチェス首相が率いる中道左派の与党・社会労働党（PSOE）の支持率は低迷している（図表 1）。

もともと PSOE の人気は低迷しており、前回 2023 年 7 月の総選挙でも、ライバル政党である中道右派の国民
党（PP）が第一党となっていた。しかしサンチェス首相は他の左派政党や地域政党の協力を受けて組閣し、政権
を維持した経緯がある。しかし首相周辺の汚職疑惑などから PSOE の人気は回復せず、次期の総選挙では PP
と同根の右派政党である Vox による連立政権が成立する公算が大きい。 
図表 1. 主要政党の支持率調査 図表 2. スペインの住宅価格

（出所）各種世論調査より

ユーロ圏 スペイン
（2015年=100、季調済）
（年、四半期）
〇スペインのサンチェス首相が10月5日、議会を解散し、11月29日に総選挙を行うと発表した。次期の総選挙
では野党第一党の PP と同根の右派政党 Vox による連立政権が成立する公算が大きい。 
〇PP と Vox が総選挙で勝利すれば、2018年6月以来の政権交代が実現する。こうした右派回帰を民族主義
の台頭という流れに位置付ける向きもあるが、スペインの国内事情はもっと複雑である。 
〇仮に総選挙を経て PP と Vox の連立政権が成立しても、両派間ですり合わせるべき課題も多く、スペイン政
治が安定するとは限らない。ゆえに、当面の問題への対応も進まないだろう。

### Page 2

解散の直接的な理由となった住宅価格の問題であるが、住宅市況が底にあった 2015 年を基準（＝100）とす
る指数でその動きを確認すると、直近 2026 年 4～6 月期は 201 と、ユーロ圏全体（159）を大きく上回る（図表 2）。
特に 2023 年以降の上昇が目覚ましいが、堅調な景気や移民の急増を受けて住宅需要が高まったことがその主
因である。

2. 民族主義的な右派回帰とは異なる 
PP と Vox が総選挙で勝利すれば、2018 年 6 月以来の政権交代が実現する。こうした右派回帰を民族主義
の台頭という流れに位置付ける向きもあるが、スペインの国内事情は複雑であるため、必ずしもその流れに合わ
ない点、留意すべきである。まず、そもそも PSOE の人気そのものが低下している。確かに景気は堅調だが、住
宅価格の急騰のような社会問題に関して、サンチェス政権は具体的な処方箋を提示できなかった。

総選挙を経て政権交代が実現し、PP が Vox と連立を組んだとしても、それを民族主義の台頭の表れと理解す
るよりも、一定の期間ごとに繰り返される左派から右派へのリバランスの流れとして理解するほうが適切だろう。ま
たスペインの場合、右派間に対立関係があることにも注目する必要がある。地域ナショナリズムが強いスペインで
は地方分権を求める保守の力が強く、中央集権を主張する保守（つまり Vox）と対立関係にある。

中央集権を望む保守の一部に戦前・戦後のフランコ体制を理想化する向きがあることも、地域ナショナリズムと
の対立を生んでいる。地域ナショナリズム政党の中には、自らを保守と位置付けながらも、中央集権を望む保守と
の対立から、PSOE や他の左派政党（Podemos や Sumar）と歩調を合わせる動きもある。スペインの民族主義は
多様なため、同国の右派回帰を反欧州連合（EU）や反移民の文脈で捉えることはできない。

例えばスペイン南東部のカタルーニャ州では、伝統的に左派も右派も民族主義を掲げ、独立運動（あるいは高
度な自治の獲得のための運動）を展開しているが、同時に票を奪い合う関係にある。最近は左派のカタルーニャ
共和主義左翼（ERC）が劣勢である。一方、右派のカタルーニャ同盟（AC）が優勢となっており、政党支持率では
PSOE に次ぐ第 2 位となっている。

AC は右派だが独立を主張しており、中央集権を主張する Vox とは対立する。一方、カタルーニャ州でも独立
に同調しない有権者は数多く、そうした有権者は PSOE に投票する傾向が強い。カタルーニャ州はスペインでも
有数の産業地帯であるため、労働運動が盛んだったことから、伝統的に PSOE の支持が厚いためである。このよ
うに、スペイン政治はまだら模様であるため、右派回帰と民族主義を同一視することは不適切である。

### Page 3

3. 総選挙後の新政権も多難な船出となる見込み 
総選挙後にいかなる立場の新政権が成立しようとも、スペイン経済の先行きに対する懸念は残る。確かに景気
は堅調を維持しているが、一方、冒頭で述べた住宅価格の高騰のような社会問題に対する妙薬はなかなか存在
しない。加えて、経済成長の次なる牽引役を見いだせていないという問題もある。例えばスペイン景気を牽引する
輸出だが、旅行サービスに支えられたサービスの輸出は好調でもモノの輸出は不調である（図表 3）。

中南米からの移民による労働供給が潤沢とはいえ、グローバルな労働力不足やスペインの人口構成（純増で
はあるが移民流入に依存した増加）に鑑みれば、スペインでも生産性の向上は不可欠である。それに資する総固
定資本形成は、実体として建設投資に偏っており、機械投資や知的財産投資の規模は限定的である（図表 4）。
これらの投資を増やすためには国による産業政策が欠かせない。

具体的には、規制緩和や税制優遇といった措置である。これまではサンチェス首相率いる PSOE 政権が少数
与党だったということや、PSOE が中道左派であるため経済成長よりも所得分配を重視したこともあり、そうした設
備投資を後押しするような政策が必ずしも打ち出されたわけではなかった。では PP と Vox の連立政権に交代し
たことでそれが可能になるかどうかというと、その可能性も高くない。

なぜならば、PP と Vox の連立政権が強いイニシアチブの下で産業政策を“上から”推し進めようとすると、中央
集権的な色彩が強くなり、地域ナショナリズムとの間であつれきが生じる展開が予想されるためである。中央集権
志向を弱めるという現実的な判断を Vox ができるかどうかが鍵を握るが、同党にはもともと PP から右派色の濃い
グループが離脱して結党した経緯があるだけに、その余地がどの程度か定かではない。

現状では総選挙を経て PSOE から PP と Vox の連立へと政権が交代する公算が大きいが、両党ですり合わせ
るべき課題も多く、政治が安定するとは限らない。ゆえに、当面の問題への対応も進まないだろう。 
図表 3. GDP に占める輸出の割合 図表 4. GDP に占める固定資本形成の割合

Document 496: 不動産市場で存在感を増す私募ファンド ～多様な運用戦略とREITとの補完関係～

Source PDF: all report pdf/不動産市場で存在感を増す私募ファンド ～多様な運用戦略とREITとの補完関係～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-11-06
Pages: 4
Status: ok

### Page 1

Flash Insights

2026 年 11 月 6 日

1/4 
不動産市場で存在感を増す私募ファンド 
～多様な運用戦略と REIT との補完関係～ 
 資産運用調査部 シニア研究員  河谷 善夫

1．大きく残高を増やしてきた私募ファンド 
資料 1 は、我が国の不動産証券化の主体別保有資産額の推移と、2015 年度から 2025 年度までの
年平均増加率（CAGR）を示したものである。

資料 1 不動産証券化の主体別保有資産額の推移（左）と年平均増加率（CAGR）（右） 
<保有資産額の推移>    <年平均増加率（CAGR）>

（注 1）J-REIT、私募リート、不動産特定共同事業は各年度末、私募ファンドは各年度の 12 月末時点。年平均増加率は、それぞ
れの系列の 2015 年度と 2025 年度の比較値から算出。なお、資産額は、保有する不動産・信託受益権の残高を示し、年間の
取得額や投資家の出資額ではない。 
（注 2）不動産特定共同事業とは、不動産特定共同事業法に基づき、国土交通大臣や都道府県知事などの許可・登録を受けて
行われるもの。複数の投資家から出資金を募り、不動産を売買・賃貸・運用して得られた利益を分配する事業。不動産クラウド
ファンディングの多くが活用し、少額からの投資が可能となっている。 
（注 3）不動産私募ファンドには、広義には私募リートも含まれる。私募リートは、投信法に基づく非上場の投資法人である。運用
期間を定めず、一定の条件の下で投資口の払戻しに応じる（オープンエンド）。一方、それ以外の私募ファンドには、合同会社
や特定目的会社等を用い、運用期間を定め、投資家の請求による期中の払戻しを行わないものが多い。本稿では、私募リート
を除く不動産私募ファンドを「私募ファンド」と呼ぶ。なお、私募リート以外の私募ファンドにも、無期限・オープンエンド型の商品
がある。 
（出所）国土交通省「不動産証券化の実態調査」及びARES J-REIT Databook（2026 年）を基に第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作
成

2015 年度時点の比較では、J-REITの保有資産額が最も大きかったが、2020 年代に入り、私募フ
ァンド、私募リートの保有資産額は、J-REITを上回るペースで増加している。 
2025 年度時点でみると、私募ファンドの保有資産額は約39.5 兆円（2025 年 12 月末）と、J-

### Page 2

2/4 
REITの約24.5 兆円（2026 年3 月末）を約 15 兆円上回っている。この間の年平均増加率で最も大
きいのは、私募リートだが、私募ファンドも年11％を超える伸びを示した。 
もっとも、保有資産には借入金を活用して取得したものも含まれるため、その残高増加を、投
資家からの新規出資額の増加と同一視することはできない。また、残高の増加だけで運用成績を
判断することもできない。一方、不動産の保有・運用を担う主体として、私募ファンドの存在感
が増していることは確認できる。近年の拡大ペースの違いを考えるうえでは、資金調達経路の違
いも重要である。J-REITでは、投資口価格の低迷が公募増資を通じた物件取得の制約となり得
る。実際、2025 年は、投資口価格が 1 口当たりの純資産価値（NAV）を下回る銘柄が多く、公募増
資が低調だった。一方、私募ファンドは、投資家の意向に応じた投資対象・運用戦略を設定して
出資を募る仕組みであり、上場市場とは異なる経路で不動産投資資金を受け入れている。こうし
た違いが、近年の資産拡大ペースの差に関係している可能性がある。 
本稿では、私募ファンドの仕組みや運用戦略を整理し、REITとの役割の違いを踏まえて、今後
の注目点を示す。

2．私募ファンドの仕組みと資金の出し手 
私募ファンドは、特定の投資家から集めた出資金と金融機関からの借入金等を用いて、不動産
を取得・運用する仕組みである。代表的な形態には、合同会社が匿名組合出資を受けるGK-TKスキ
ーム（資料2 左）や、特定目的会社を用いるTMKスキーム（資料2 右）がある。運用会社（アセッ
トマネジメント会社）が投資対象や運用方針に応じて物件の取得・管理・売却等を行う。

資料 2 私募ファンドの仕組みの概要 
<GK-TKスキームの例>    <TMKスキームの例>

（注）匿名組合出資は、合同会社が行う事業に出資し、その利益の分配を受ける契約に基づく出資。優先出資は、TMKの特定出
資に対し、利益配当又は残余財産の分配で優先する出資であり、借入金・社債より優先するものではない。特定出資等は図示を
省略。 
（出所）第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成

GK-TKスキームでは、合同会社が投資家からの匿名組合出資と借入金により、通常、不動産信託
受益権を取得する。不動産自体は信託銀行等が所有し、合同会社はその収益等を受け取る。一
方、TMKスキームでは、特定目的会社が優先出資や借入金・社債等によって資金を調達し、不動産
を直接所有するか、不動産信託受益権を保有する。

### Page 3

3/4 
私募ファンドは、こうした法的な仕組みだけでなく、運用戦略や投資目的も多様である。資料 3
では、その代表例を整理する。

資料 3 私募ファンドの主な運用戦略・目的

（注）代表例を示したものであり、各区分は必ずしも相互に排他的ではない。 
（出所）第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成

私募ファンドに出資する投資家としては、国内外の年金基金、保険会社、金融機関、政府系フ
ァンド、事業会社等がある。 
こうした資金を受け入れるファンドの運用戦略は、資料 3 に示したように、安定した賃料収入
を重視するものから、開発等による価値向上を目指すものまで幅広い。このように私募ファンド
は、単にJ-REITより流動性が低い投資商品というだけでなく、投資対象、保有期間、開発・改修
の有無等に応じた多様な投資機会を提供するものといえる。 
流動性の低さは投資上の制約であるが、長期資金を持つ投資家にとっては、それだけで投資対
象から外れるわけではない。投資対象や運用戦略を選択し、専門的な運用を通じて収益を獲得す
る手段として、私募ファンドが活用されている。

3．REITとの役割の違いと今後の注目点 
J-REITや私募リートが主として不動産の継続的な保有・運用による賃料収入を重視するのに対
し、私募ファンドには、開発や改修による価値向上と、その後の売却を組み合わせるものもあ
る。長期保有型も含む多様な戦略の中で、開発・価値向上段階のリスクを引き受ける資金の受け
皿となっている点に、私募ファンドの役割の一つがある。 
私募ファンドのうち、あらかじめ運用期間を定める有期ファンドでは、物件売却による資金回
収を予定する場合、その時期も運用計画に組み込まれる。契約等に基づいて運用期間を延長でき
る場合もあるが、延長の可否・条件や、必要に応じた借換えへの対応を考慮する必要がある。こ
のため、運用期限を設けないJ-REITや私募リートとは異なり、想定した時期・価格で売却できる
かに加え、売却を延期する場合の条件にも注意が必要である。 
また、私募ファンドの中には、将来のREIT等への売却を想定して物件を一時的に保有する「ブ
リッジファンド」もある。REITへの売却を想定するこうしたファンドでは、売却先となるREITの
取得余力も重要となる。このため、私募ファンドの拡大を、REITに代わる投資先の成長とのみ捉
えるのは十分ではない。開発や価値向上を担い、REIT等の長期保有主体に物件を供給する補完的

### Page 4

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
4/4 
な役割にも目を向ける必要がある。 
今後は、私募ファンドの資金調達環境に加え、運用戦略の実行可能性や売却先となるREIT等の
取得余力にも着目し、市場拡大の持続性を見極めていくことが重要である。

十一月 09, 2026 研究網站:  https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R Powered by the EFA Platform 1

動態更新 自行車 ‧ 台灣

巨大 
(9921.TW/9921 TT)

邁向新一輪成長週期 
重要訊息 
3Q26 EPS  0.84 元，分別較市場共識及我們預估低 23%及 40%，主因行銷
費用增加及美國進口禁令相關法律費用，完全抵銷毛利率優於預期的影響。
展望後市，我們認為需求前景改善，加上有利的利潤率結構，將支撐未來獲
利穩健復甦。 
評論及分析 
3Q26 獲利落後，主因營業費用較高。受惠歐洲及中國需求改善，以及平均
售價回升，3Q26 營收年增 6.2%，連續第二季恢復年成長，表現大致符合
我們預期。毛利率 23.9%，年增 2.4 個百分點，遠優於市場共識的 21.5%
及我們預估的 22.6%，主要受惠自有品牌銷售比重提升，以及庫存去化結束
後折扣幅度縮減。然而，新車款上市所帶來的較高行銷費用，加上約 3,000
萬元的美國進口禁令相關法律費用，使營業費用率升至 21.3%，顯著高於我
們預估。因此營益率季減 2.9 個百分點至 3.1%，遠低於市場共識的 3.9%
與我們預估的 4.0%。 
利潤率趨勢改善。截至 3Q26，公司庫存週轉天數進一步降至 145 天，年減
2%，且低於歷史平均水準 148 天，顯示終端需求持續改善，產品平均售價
趨勢亦轉趨有利。搭配新車款推出，我們認為利潤率復甦趨勢穩健。雖然美
國進口禁令仍為短期隱憂，對自有品牌銷售及代工訂單造成壓力，但我們認
為巨大多元化生產基地佈局多少能降低相關衝擊。我們預估 4Q26 合併營收
將年增 7%，延續成長動能，且毛利率亦可望持續年增至 22.0%。 
獲利復甦趨勢無虞。儘管公司維持 2026 年營運展望不變，包括營收低個位
數衰退、毛利率 21-23%，以及營益率 1-3%，我們認為受惠產品組合優化
及營運槓桿改善，利潤率可望落在區間上檔。展望 2027 年，我們預期終端
需求復甦將帶動營收重回成長軌道，年增 7%；隨著毛利率進一步改善，每
股盈餘可望年增 102%至 5.98 元，我們認為利潤率及獲利持續復甦將帶動
評價進一步提升。 
投資建議 
儘管 3Q26 財報落後，我們認為巨大營收及利潤率復甦趨勢顯示自行車產業
2027 年將邁入新一輪成長週期。隨著獲利能見度提高，可望帶動個股評價
回升。我們維持「增加持股」評等及 120 元目標價，係根據 20 倍本益比。 
投資風險 
下檔風險包括需求復甦放緩與中國價格競爭壓力。

圖 4: 公司概況  圖 5: 歐洲為營收最大來源 
巨大成立於 1972 年，為台灣第一大自行車製造商。目前生產基
地位於中國、歐洲 (荷蘭及匈牙利) 與臺灣。歐洲為最大市場，
2025 年營收佔比達 42%，中國營收貢獻 26%，美洲為 9%，台
灣 8%，日本 6%及其他 9%。

### Page 1

十一月 11, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R 1

法說會重點摘要

藥華藥 
(6446 TT, NT$1,335, 增加持股)

重點摘要 
1. 藥華藥 3Q26 營收 77.6 億元，QoQ +13% ，YoY 近翻倍，主因美
國與日本銷量成長、Ropeg 注射筆於美國上市，以及美日原發性血
小板過多症（ET）適應症自 9M26 開始貢獻；1-10M26 營收 226
億元，YoY +86% 。3Q26 毛利率 96.8%，YoY +5.2ppts ；營益率
40.3%，排除約 4.7 億元員工認購庫藏股費用後約 46.4%；EPS 
7.08 元創單季新高，1-3Q26 EPS 18.43 元已達 2025 年全年的 1.5
倍 
2. ET 已取得台灣、日本、美國、中國藥證，美日皆為全線適應症，
韓國預計 2027 年初取證；真性紅血球增多症（PV）方面，Ropeg
於 NCCN 指南高、低風險皆列首選，競品 rusfertide 僅列註腳。我
們認為 ET 可直接沿用 PV 既有的醫師網絡與用藥經驗，放量速度可
望快於 PV 上市初期，為 2027 年營收成長主軸；公司目標 2027 年
費用率降至 40 幾%，惟投資抵減用完後稅率將朝 20%靠近 
3. 原發性骨髓纖維化（PMF）三期試驗 HOPE-PMF 已於 6M26 完成收
案 160 人、9M26 通過資料安全監測委員會（DSMB）審查，目標
2028 年解盲送件，為第三個適應症；CML、HCC、AML、CTCL 等
研究者發起試驗（IIT）持續延伸適應症。2026-2027 年多項 PEG
細胞激素、雙特異性抗體、ADC、MPN 標靶新藥與 TCR-T 目標申
請 IND；竹北廠目標 2027 年投產 
結論 
我們認為藥華藥已進入 PV、ET 雙適應症驅動的成長階段，3Q26 在一
次性費用干擾下營益率仍達 40.3%，營業槓桿持續發揮。我們看好 ET
藉 NCCN 首選地位與既有骨髓增生性腫瘤（MPN）醫師網絡加速滲
透，帶動 2027 年營收增速持續高於費用；惟稅率回升將使淨利增幅低
於營益。後續驗證點為 4Q26 首個 ET 完整季度貢獻、2027 年費用率
能否降至 40 幾%，以及 2028 年 HOPE-PMF 解盲結果

3Q26 財務表現 
 3Q26 營收 77.6 億元，QoQ +13% ，YoY 近翻倍，YoY 增幅由
2Q26 約 +90%擴大至接近+100%；成長來自美國與日本銷量成
長、注射筆於美國上市，以及美日 ET 適應症自 9M26 開始貢獻 
 3Q26 毛利率 96.8%，YoY +5.2ppts，原因有三： 
 美國、日本兩大高價值市場營收占比提高，產品組合優化 
 美國藥價於 5M26 調升，3Q26 為首個完整反映的季度 
 銷售以美元為主、成本以新台幣為主，新台幣較去年同期走貶

請將您珍貴的一票投給凱基！

### Page 2

台灣 藥華藥

十一月 11, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R 2

 3Q26 營業費用 YoY +83% ，主因認列員工認購庫藏股費用約 4.7
億元（非經常性），排除後 YoY +63% ；費用率由 3Q25 的 61.6%
降至 56.5%，排除後約 50.4% 
 3Q26 營業利益 31.3 億元，YoY +168% ；營益率 40.3%，YoY 
+10.4ppts，排除庫藏股費用後約 46.4%，與 2Q26 相當 
 3Q26 稅後淨利 29.2 億元，YoY +98%，淨利率 37.7%；淨利增幅
低於營益，主因 3Q25 認列所得稅利益墊高基期。3Q26 有效稅率
約 10%來自投資抵減，預計 2026 年大致用完，2027 年稅率朝
20%靠近 
 3Q26 EPS 7.08 元，YoY +97% ，創單季新高；1-3Q26 營收 YoY 
+84%，營業利益 83.7 億元，YoY +149% ，EPS 18.43 元，已為
2025 年全年 12.3 元的 1.5 倍 
 1-10M26 營收 226 億元，YoY +86% ，2025 年全年營收為 157 億
元

PV：NCCN 首選地位穩固 
 美國 MPN 病患約 34.7 萬人，其中 PV 約 16.3 萬人、ET 約 17 萬
人、PMF 約 1.4 萬人；ET 人數隨診斷率提升持續增加 
 PV 已擴展至全球約 50 國，2026 年取得加拿大藥證，後續包括墨
西哥、越南、哥倫比亞 
 NCCN PV 指南中，Ropeg 為低風險 PV 唯一首選療法、高風險 PV
唯一首選干擾素 
 Takeda／Protagonist 的 rusfertide 於 2026/8/28 獲准用於 PV，但
NCCN 僅列於註腳： 
 限頻繁放血或不耐受放血時使用，高風險病人只能 add-on 於現
行治療 
 機制為降低鐵的利用、只降低紅血球生成，不能取代細胞減量療
法；Ropeg 試驗評估的完全血液學反應（CHR）涵蓋紅血球、白
血球與血小板 
 低風險 PV 中 Ropeg 合併放血對照僅放血：治療反應率 81.3%對
50.8%，血球比容（Hct）控制 81.3%對 58.7%，疾病惡化 0%對
12.7%；KOL 愈來愈重視疾病修飾與早期治療，有助擴大可治療族
群

ET：四國取證、全線適應症 
 過去 ET 用藥僅 Hydroxyurea（ HU）與 anagrelide，臨床試驗中
Ropeg 對比 anagrelide 為 42.9%對 6%，達統計顯著 
 ET 藥證：台灣 2026/5/12、日本 8/24、美國 8/28、中國 10/15 已
取證；新加坡 10/12 送件；韓國預計 2027 年初取證 
 美日皆取得全線 ET 適應症（一線、二線病人皆可使用），後續各國
送件亦朝全線方向；日本 ET 病患約 6.3 萬人、美國約 17 萬人 
 Ropeg 注射筆已於美國上市並貢獻 10M26 營收，病人與醫師回饋

### Page 3

台灣 藥華藥

十一月 11, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R 3

良好，有助提升病患留存與新病患採用

PMF 三期與適應症延伸 
 HOPE-PMF 為隨機、雙盲、安慰劑對照三期試驗，2:1 分組，治療
80 週進行指標分析： 
 6M26 完成收案，超收至 160 人 
 9M26 通過 DSMB 審查，目標 2028 年解盲並送件 
 PMF IIT 兩年數據：血液學反應率 W12 為 61.4% 、 W48 為
63.5%，其後達 70.0%；平均血小板由 512 降至 279（×10⁹/L ），
W12 起即低於正常值上限 
 IIT 延伸：CML（併用 dasatinib）一期／二期 IND 已獲核准、HCC
（併用 anti-PD-1）一期數據已發表、AML 一期 IIT 進行中、CTCL
與 CMML 二期 IND 準備中、MF（併用 ruxolitinib）一期 IND 已提
交；NCCN CTCL 指南已將 Ropeg 列為干擾素替代選項

早期產品線與產能 
 2026-2027 年多項產品目標進入臨床一期： 
 雙特異性抗體 PD-1-IL-2v 預計 2026 年 IND 
 PEG-IL-2、PEG-Cytokines、ADC 與 KRAS TCR -T 預計 2027 年
IND 
 MPN 新藥 Type 2 JAK2 inhibitor 與 mCALR antibody 預計 2027
年 IND 
 已併購加拿大 FORUS，並於墨爾本設立營運據點推動臨床試驗；
2026 年全球員工目標接近 1,000 人 
 竹北廠已進入製程設備安裝與機電管線整合階段，目標 2027 年投
產；波多黎各廠設備均已下單，相關申請進行中

QA 
 CMS 追溯調整何時認列完畢？ 
 2025 年 CMS 追溯調整預期於 2026 年處理完畢，剩餘金額較前
兩次小；調整皆反映於營收，不影響營業成本 
 2027 年費用率如何展望？ 
 1-3Q26 費用率約 51%，2027 年 PV、ET 雙適應症帶動營收成長
高於費用成長，目標費用率往 40 幾%邁進 
 不提供推銷、管理、研發細項預估，建議參考 1Q-2Q26 的費用
結構 
 稅率展望為何？ 
 3Q26 有效稅率 10%主要來自投資抵減，預計 2026 年大致用
完，2027 年稅率朝 20%靠近

台灣 藥華藥

十一月 11, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R 4

 何時能達到規模經濟？ 
 3Q26 營益率 40.3%，國際大藥廠平均約 30%，前段班約 40-
55%，禮來約 50 幾%，公司仍有優化空間 
 資本配置規劃為何？ 
 竹北、波多黎各廠依計畫擴建，並於美國購置不動產作為未來辦
公室 
 積極評估併購、少數股權投資與授權，並投資基金尋找合適標的 
 在保留發展資金的前提下，以股利回饋股東 
 ET 在美國的給付與藥價狀況？ 
 ET 適應症已納入美國保險給付體系，處方採用持續增加 
 ET 與 PV 為同一產品，批發標價統一，實際淨價依各保險支付方
的合約與回饋而異 
 ET 成長速度是否會快於 PV？ 
 9M26 即有實質營收貢獻；MPN 醫師手上同時有 PV 與 ET 病
人，且已熟悉 Ropeg 的安全性，加上醫學科學聯絡官（MSL）團
隊支援，拓展速度將優於 PV 上市初期 
 PV 與 ET 互相帶動，並已耕耘社區醫院，加速處方滲透 
 是否考慮布局調控鐵的藥品？ 
 公司認為調控鐵僅降低紅血球生成，產業趨勢是疾病修飾、降低
突變等位基因負荷，因此布局 Type 2 JAK2 與 mCALR 等標靶藥
物 
 Ropeg 在中國的定價策略？ 
 中國醫保走向較 commodity，為避免影響國際定價，目前以自費
市場為主，並持續與主管機關溝通

法說會議重點摘要

博智 
(8155 TT, NT$255, 未評等)

重點摘要 
1. 3Q26毛利率季增 3 ppts 至 26.9%，來自於高階產品出貨比重拉升，
漲價效應。 
2. 2027 年展望，AI Server 增加、 IPC: B/B >1、IC Tester: 機種、客戶增
加。 
3. 2028 年 AI Server 需求持續強勁， IC Tester 比例增加，低軌衛星與太
空 AI 伺服器專案，博智具備極高機會參與高階板材製造。 
結論 
我們認為公司在 AI 伺服器與高毛利 IC Tester 比重提升下將大幅受惠，目
前股價交易於 2027 市場共識之每股盈餘的 11.3 倍。 
營運概況 
 1Q-3Q26 累計合併營收達 48 億元，較 2025 年同期成長 35%。
2026 年單季營收呈逐季增長，累計 1Q-3Q26 年增達三成以上。累
計每股盈餘達到 11.15 元。3Q26 毛利率跳升，因高階產品出貨比
重拉升，且成功將上游 CCL 材料漲價順利轉嫁給客戶。 
 2026 產品結構，Server 營收占比 82%、IC tester 9% 、IPC 7%及
Networking 1%。 
 2026 板層數營收佔比：14-20L、 22-34+L、 2-12L 以上佔比為
50%、27%、23%。 
 博智核心技術：  
 實際量產中非對稱堆疊（N+M）設計顯著增加，極易引發板彎
翹。 
 需在高層數、大尺寸及板彎翹條件下精準控制背鑽深度，加工數
千至數萬個不同深度之孔位。公司具備高重橫比電鍍及 Multi-
layer 集成能力。 
 博智核心 SI（訊號完整性）模擬實力：，CCL 材料交期拉長
時，博智憑藉強大的 SI 模擬能力，能協助客戶快速尋找並驗證 
Second source 備用材料，大幅降低認證門檻與缺料衝擊。 
 目標市場現況：  
 Server：主流聚焦約 8-30 層板，排版尺寸最大達 22x27 吋，板
厚不超過 5mm，採用 M6-7 材料。受限於現有產能，優先配置
生產非高階 HDI 之 N+M 及 2/3 次壓合高層板。 
 IPC：應用於工業、醫療與機器人，層數 2-26 層，尺寸 20x24 
吋，板厚約 3.0mm，採用 M4-6 材料。(訂單能見度:6-12 個月
訂單)。

置節奏稍微放緩，但 AI 總體需求強勁，長遠發展更趨健康。AI 產
業榮景估計仍有 10 至 15 年長路。

Document 701: 米中間選挙で民主党が大勝 ～出口調査からみる共和党の敗因～

Source PDF: all report pdf/米中間選挙で民主党が大勝 ～出口調査からみる共和党の敗因～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-11-04
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

US Trends

2026年 11月 4日

1/3 
米中間選挙で民主党が大勝 
～出口調査からみる共和党の敗因～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  前田 和馬 
（℡：050-5473-3888）

11 月3 日に投開票を迎えた米国中間選挙において、民主党が上下院の過半数獲得を確実とし
た。New York Timesの4 日午前 3 時（米東部時間）時点の予測では、民主党の議席数は上院で 55
（選挙前：47）、下院で257（同 214）と大幅に議席を伸ばした見込みだ。CNNによる投票者調査
（事前調査と出口調査による集計：Voter polls）に基づくと、今回の選挙結果の背景として下記
3 点を指摘できる。なお、2024 年大統領選との比較に関してはサンプル規模など調査手法が異な
る点に留意が必要である。

① 若者や中南米系が共和党から離反 
 共和党は白人男性層に支持が多い一方、女性や若者は相対的に民主党を支持する傾向が強い。
2024 年大統領選と比べると、今回の中間選挙ではあらゆる属性で民主党の得票率が伸びており、
特に 18～29 歳の若者層や中南米系で上昇幅が大きい（図表 1）。 これらの有権者層は所得が相対的
に低く、住宅所有率も低いため、物価高に対するトランプ政権への不満が投票行動に反映されや
すかったとみられる。

② 物価高に対する不満 
「生活費の問題が投票に重要（主要）な影響を与えた」との回答は 89%に達し、経済や物価高の
問題が選挙における最大の争点の一つであった。なお、生活費以外の投票要因では政府の汚職が
90%に達し、イラン戦争は 74%、移民取締政策が73%、AI・データセンターが 56%と続く。なお、汚
職への高い回答割合は物価高への不満によって増長された可能性があり、2028 年大統領選でもこ
れが重要な争点となるかは不透明感が残る。 
経済運営を巡って、2024 年大統領選の際には 53%の有権者が「トランプ氏の経済運営の方が信
頼できる」と回答するなど（民主党・ハリス氏は 46%）、トランプ大統領の再選の背景には生活改
善への強い期待があった。一方、今回の中間選挙では「トランプ政策で経済環境が悪化した」と
の回答が65%に達するなど、高関税政策による輸入物価の上昇やイラン戦争によるガソリン価格高
騰への強い不満がみられた。

### Page 2

2/3 
③ 消去法的な民主党への投票 
「民主党を好ましい」とみる有権者は 47%（共和党：同 41%）と、「好ましくない」の 53%を下回
っている。また、全有権者の 18%は「民主党をやや好ましくない」と回答したものの、このうち
46%が民主党候補に投票している。すなわち、全有権者の8%程度は消去法的に民主党候補を選んだ
様子がみてとれる。加えて、全有権者の12%は「社会主義をやや好ましくない」と回答した一方、
このうち71%は民主党候補に投票している。このため、2028 年大統領選までに民主党が左派色を
強める場合、こうした投票者層は再び共和党へ投票先を変更する可能性がある。

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
3/3

・今後の政策見通し 
 民主党は上下院で勝利したものの、トランプ大統領は議会が可決した法案への拒否権を有して
いる。拒否権を覆すには上下両院でそれぞれ 3 分の 2 以上の賛成が必要であり、共和党議員の大
幅な造反がなければ実現は難しいだろう。一方、トランプ政権は議会との対立が強まるなか、大
統領令を活用すると同時に、大統領権限が強い外交や関税政策でのレガシー作りを目指すとみら
れる。イラン戦争は議会による国防予算への権限、関税政策は司法判断がどの程度の歯止めにな
るかが注目される。他方、AI規制は連邦レベルでの規制案が合意に達するか不透明であり、州・
地方政府レベルでのデータセンター建設規制等が続く可能性がある（詳細は 2026 年 10 月28 日付
け「米国：民主党勝利で政策は変わるのか？」）。

図表 4：主要政策の見通し

出所: 第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所が作成

以 上 
①イラン情勢
・
・
・
トランプ政権は外交でのレガシー作りを目指す
トランプ政権がガソリン高に配慮する動機は選挙後に減少
議会は国防予算の承認を通じて戦争終結へ圧力
②関税政策
・
・
・
選挙後に更なる追加関税策が実行に移される懸念
民主党は関税政策の撤廃を目指すものの、大統領の拒否権を覆
すには超党派での対応が必要
関税の持続性は司法判断に依存
③AI規制
・
・
・
トランプ政権は対中競争力の観点から、AI規制に消極的
連邦レベルのAI規制に関しては合意まで距離
州・地方政府によるDC建設規制が続く可能性
④金融政策
・
・
・
短期的な政策見通しは経済・物価動向に依存
2027～28年にトランプ政権は1～2名のFRB理事を指名する可能性
ただ、後任者は民主党支配の上院で承認が必要
⑤財政政策
・
・
・
基本的に財政スタンスの大幅な変化は見込みにくい
民主党は医療・社会保障支出の確保を優先
予算審議が難航すれば、政府閉鎖や債務上限問題が浮上

Document 727: 美国10月CPI：通胀风险，刻不容缓——2026年10月美国CPI数据点评

Source PDF: all report pdf/美国10月CPI：通胀风险，刻不容缓——2026年10月美国CPI数据点评.pdf
Report source: 东吴证券
Date: 2026-11-11
Pages: 7
Status: ok

### Page 1

证券研究报告·宏观报告·宏观点评

东吴证券研究所

2026年11月11日

相关研究  
《10月美国CPI前瞻：油价与经济韧
性的双重检验——海外周报
20261109》 
2026-11-09 
《食品价格回落或影响 10月CPI同
比读数》 
2026-11-08

[Table_Tag]    [Table_Summary] 
◼ 核心观点：10月美国CPI再度全面超预期，通胀扩散度连续第三个月
走高，核心通胀3个月均年率升至3.68%的高位，这继续强化了9月美
国CPI释放的美国经济韧性和油价传导信号。展望看，当前市场对未来
通胀路径仍然偏乐观，因美国通胀问题不在于油价短期的边际变化，而
是7月以来油价“Higher for Longer”所引发的通胀扩散风险。对于货币
政策而言，仅从泰勒规则来看，当前市场定价的加息预期仍有加码空间；
而12月最终是否落地50bps加息，短期来看油价仍是关键。对于交易
策略而言，9—10月核心CPI连续超预期的启示在于，需要更加重视通
胀扩散风险。我们认为，除非中东的能源供给风险得到实质性化解，否
则当前权益投资者相对于FICC投资者更加乐观的定价存在较大的纠偏
风险。 
◼ 美国10月CPI：再度超预期。10月CPI环比+0.35%，预期+0.3%，前
值+0.65%；核心CPI环比+0.30%，预期+0.2%，前值+0.33%。这是美国
核心CPI环比连续第三个月超预期，核心CPI环比3个月均年率也从7
月的+1.63%走高至10月的+3.68%。数据公布后，市场交易典型的紧货
币，美债利率走高、黄金&铜&油走低。由于数据发布前，美联储理事
Waller表示若10月核心CPI环比达到0.3%甚至更高，不排除12月加
息50bps的可能性，市场对12月加息预期50bps的定价一度达到14%。 
◼ 通胀结构：核心分项环比全面走高，通胀广度扩散。从分项看，与9月
CPI数据类似，10月美国CPI的几大核心项目环比均维持在较高水平，
通胀扩散度继续走高。①核心商品方面，10月核心商品CPI环比+0.16%，
其中除二手车以外核心商品环比+0.39%，为2023年1月以来最高，前
值+0.18%。趋势上，核心商品环比已连续4个月为正；结构上，全球供
应链压力、关税和AI相关商品涨价的影响仍在持续。其中，高占比和
高波动的二手车环比跌至-1.22%，与其领先指标Manheim二手车连续
几个月的回落趋势一致。其他商品类别中，家居用品环比大幅升至
+0.71%，其中的家务商品、家具&床上用品环比增长加速，但持续性有
待验证；医保、休闲、教育&通讯商品等环比增速均录得0.5%—0.7%的
较高区间，反映关税或油价二轮效应的广泛性。②居住通胀方面，10月
核心服务环比+0.34%，前值+0.37%，其中居住通胀环比+0.30%，较前值
+0.39%略有回落。主要分项中，住所租金和自住房折算环比分别录得
+0.20%、+0.32%，虽然相较上月的高位有所回落，但较此前0.1%—0.2%
中枢仍偏高；高波动的酒店住宿环比回落至+0.64%。从领先指标——领
先约16个月的房价和领先约6—8个月的租金来看，居住通胀的趋势下
行或已接近尾声。③超级核心通胀方面，10月非居住核心服务通胀环比
+0.41%，前值+0.34%，超级核心通胀（包括居住服务中的酒店住宿）环
比+0.40%，前值+0.42%，均保持在高中枢，且 3个月均年率分别录得
+4.68%、+5.31%的高位。尤其是，从结构来看，以往的高波动、易带来
噪音的项目如机票、酒店住宿、车险等本月均没有异常偏高，表明核心
服务的走高具有广泛性。④通胀扩散度方面，反映通胀广度的通胀扩散
指数在10月继续走高，环比折年增速大于过去5年均值的项目占比从
24.76%提升至32.15%，环比折年增速大于4%的项目占比从35.12%提
升至41.20%，其背后是7月以来油价“Higher for Longer”带来的更广
泛的成本走高。 
◼ 展望与策略启示：重视通胀扩散风险。总体来看，10月美国CPI连续
第3个月全面超预期，其中除二手车外的核心商品、居住服务和超级核
心通胀环比均保持在较高中枢，通胀扩散度连续第三个月上升，这继续
强化了9月美国CPI释放的信号，即美国经济韧性、油价“Higher for 
Longer”强化了通胀的传导；核心CPI连续3个月超预期且扩散风险
显著增大，使得美联储也再难以忽视通胀的上行风险。展望看，当前市
场对美国通胀路径仍然偏乐观：交易员预期明年美国 CPI环比中枢在

### Page 2

2 / 7 
0.1-0.2%，且更贴近0.1%，并预期美国CPI同比在2027年3—5月因基
数效应跌至2%。从高频预测来看，近期油价的下跌让美联储通胀实时
预测模型对11月CPI的预期并不高，但现在的问题不是油价短期的边
际变化，而是7月以来“Higher for Longer”的油价所引发的通胀扩散
风险的惯性到底多大。近期数据显示，油价的持续高企使得通胀黏性扩
大，油价带来的通胀预期上行风险正在实质化，这不是一个月油价回落
能逆转的，而是需要中东地缘风险的解除和美联储紧货币的加码。 
◼ 对于货币政策而言，加息预期仍有加码空间：超预期的10月CPI数据
与美联储官员Waller、Logan的鹰派发言后，交易员定价美联储12月、
明年9月累计加息1.1、3.0次，即12月加息50bps的概率为10%，到
明年9月累计加息3次。仅从泰勒规则来看，10月4.14%的失业率与
3.3%的核心PCE指引（根据Polymarket预测）美联储还应加息4.6—5
次，市场定价仍有上行空间。对于12月加息预期而言，最终是否落地
50bps加息，我们认为油价仍是关键：若油价“高烧难退”，通胀预期加
速上行，不排除12月“亡羊补牢”式加息50bps的可能性；不过，若风
险相对可控，12月、1月各加息25bps是为相对更稳妥的做法；另外12
月4日公布的11月非农也将影响12月FOMC与点阵图。 
◼ 对于交易策略而言，9—10月核心CPI连续超预期的启示在于，需要更
加重视通胀扩散风险。10月超预期的CPI公布后，美债、美元与黄金等
FICC市场交易紧货币预期的升温，美股则保持了相对韧性。我们认为，
除非中东的能源供给风险得到实质性化解，否则当前权益投资者相对于
FICC投资者更加乐观的定价存在较大的纠偏风险。 
◼ 风险提示：油价失控推高通胀；美联储维持高利率水平时间过长，引发
金融系统流动性危机。

免责及评级说明部分

东吴证券投资评级标准 
投资评级基于分析师对报告发布日后 6至 12个月内行业或公司回报潜力相对基准表现
的预期（A 股市场基准为沪深 300 指数，香港市场基准为恒生指数，美国市场基准为标普 
500 指数，新三板基准指数为三板成指（针对协议转让标的）或三板做市指数（针对做市转
让标的），北交所基准指数为北证 50指数），具体如下： 
公司投资评级： 
买入：预期未来 6个月个股涨跌幅相对基准在 15%以上； 
增持：预期未来 6个月个股涨跌幅相对基准介于 5%与 15%之间； 
中性：预期未来 6个月个股涨跌幅相对基准介于-5%与 5%之间； 
减持：预期未来 6个月个股涨跌幅相对基准介于-15%与-5%之间； 
卖出：预期未来 6个月个股涨跌幅相对基准在-15%以下。 
行业投资评级： 
增持： 预期未来 6个月内，行业指数相对强于基准 5%以上； 
中性： 预期未来 6个月内，行业指数相对基准-5%与 5%； 
减持： 预期未来 6个月内，行业指数相对弱于基准 5%以上。 
我们在此提醒您，不同证券研究机构采用不同的评级术语及评级标准。我们采用的是相
对评级体系，表示投资的相对比重建议。投资者买入或者卖出证券的决定应当充分考虑自身
特定状况，如具体投资目的、财务状况以及特定需求等，并完整理解和使用本报告内容，不
应视本报告为做出投资决策的唯一因素。

Document 775: 贝森特与美联储高官交锋：通胀源于能源冲击还是需求过热，美联储加息路径争论升温

Source PDF: all report pdf/贝森特与美联储高官交锋：通胀源于能源冲击还是需求过热，美联储加息路径争论升温.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-13
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

美财⻓⻉森特认为当前通胀主要源于伊朗战争引发的能源供应冲击，当前的通胀数据本质
上是 " 噪⾳ " ，美联储不应因此加息；芝加哥联储主席古尔斯⽐则认为通胀已扩散⾄服务、医
疗等领域，需求过热⻛险不容忽视，美联储仍需采取⾏动。
摘要：
美国财政部⻓与芝加哥联储主席就当前通胀根源公开交锋，折射出华盛顿与美联储之间⽇益加
深的政策分歧，并为美联储 12 ⽉议息会议蒙上更多不确定性。
美财政部⻓ Scott Bessent （⻉森特）周五接受 CNBC 采访时呼吁美联储不应因伊朗战争引发
的 " 能源供应冲击 " ⽽激进加息。
芝加哥联储主席 Austan Goolsbee （古尔斯⽐）随即在同⼀节⽬中予以回应，指出通胀已蔓延⾄
服务业、医疗等与油价关联度低的领域，并受到 AI 数据中⼼建设带动的强劲需求推升，远⾮能源
价格上涨所能解释。
Goolsbee 明确表⽰，美联储 " 还有⼤量⼯作要做 " 以重建政策公信⼒。
近期强于预期的就业与通胀数据进⼀步强化了美联储继续加息的理由，部分官员甚⾄开始讨论

在 12 ⽉ 8 ⽇⾄ 9 ⽇的会议上采取超常规的 50 个基点加息幅度。
⻉森特：能源冲击驱动通胀， Fed 不应贸然逆转
Bessent 在 CNBC 采访中将当前通胀归因于伊朗战争引发的油价上涨，并对美联储的政策⽴场提
出批评。
他表⽰，美国整体经济基本⾯依然强劲，当前的通胀数据本质上是 " 噪⾳ " ，美联储不应以此为由
⼤幅加息。
" 美联储本应着眼未来，⽽⾮盯着后视镜， "Bessent 说。他还提及美联储去年的降息举措，认为
在能源供应冲击下急剧逆转政策⽅向 " 并不是我在美联储时会做的事 " 。
他同时表⽰，⾃⼰并⾮要直接⼲预美联储决策，⽽是希望其 " 保持开放⼼态 " 。
这⼀表态与特朗普政府的整体⼝径⼀致 —— 政府官员普遍强调通胀的油价驱动属性，对美联储
的应对⽅式持批评⽴场。
古尔斯⽐：通胀已超出能源范畴，更令⼈担忧
Goolsbee 对财政部⻓的判断表⽰尊重，但明确提出了不同看法。
他指出，通胀的持续性和⼴泛性已超出能源和关税冲击所能解释的范围 —— 服务业通胀、医疗

费⽤上涨，以及 AI 数据中⼼建设带动的需求过热，均成为推⾼物价的独⽴因素。
" 关税通胀和油价冲击本应在现在消退，但它们并没有， "Goolsbee 说， " 如果需求过热并正在助
推通胀，那才是更令⼈担忧的情况。 " 他强调，服务业通胀 " ⽆论油价如何变化都不会消失 " 。

1 条评论
在通胀回归 2% ⽬标的问题上， Goolsbee 直⾔： " 我⽬前的判断是，我们尚未⾛在正确的轨道
上，必须正视这⼀现实。 " 他表⽰，即将发布的通胀数据将是判断美联储是否需要加快加息节奏
的关键依据。
研究显⽰油价与通胀的关系更为复杂
尽管特朗普政府将通胀主要归咎于油价，但据路透报道，相关研究正在揭⽰油价对美国经济影
响的另⼀⾯。
由于美国已成为能源净出⼝国，全球油价上涨不仅带来供给侧成本压⼒，还会通过企业利润提
升和⼯资增⻓等渠道向经济体内部传导，形成独⽴于供应因素之外的需求侧通胀动⼒。
这意味着，美联储难以像处理纯粹的供给冲击那样，简单地 " 穿越 " 当前通胀。
美联储官员在伊朗战争初期曾考虑对供给驱动的通胀采取观望态度，但随着通胀持续⾼于⽬

标，⽀持加息的⽴场逐渐占据上⻛。
12 ⽉会议前景：加息⼒度争议升温
近期数据正在推动市场对更激进加息路径的预期。强于预期的就业和通胀报告令部分美联储官
员开始讨论在 12 ⽉ 8 ⽇⾄ 9 ⽇会议上加息 50 个基点的可能性，⽽⾮通常的 25 个基点。
Goolsbee 虽未明确承诺加息，但措辞显⽰政策⽴场趋紧。 " 我们已连续数⽉看到通胀⼴泛化的不
良信号， " 他说， " 在与油价和关税⽆关的类别中，进展依然停滞，部分甚⾄在恶化。 "
此番政府官员与美联储之间的公开分歧，将使外界对美联储政策独⽴性的关注度持续提升， 12
⽉议息会议前的通胀及就业数据⾛势，料将成为市场聚焦的核⼼变量。
